HEADPIN.md:13:Registered before any spend. State-exact means: events are supplied (the true trace is teacher-forced) and every state token's argmax, over the full vocabulary, is the true next state. A wrong read does not by itself fail a prediction. Reads are still scored, and the first error is the earlier of a wrong read and a wrong state at that step. The margin is the target logit minus the best other logit. The minimum margin of a run is the minimum over its state tokens.
HEADPIN.md:15:**Tables.** Three families, one frozen program each: parity, cups, compass. They are single-register, so the block is one (state, event). Robot is left out because it has two registers. The program is the first training draw, seed 4040, for which `literal` accepts every tape and every sequence, including requote-256, fits in 40,960 − 64 tokens. The eight tapes at each length only resample that program's events, with generator `4040|family|length|index`. Lengths 1,024, 4,096 and 8,192. A draw the clock cannot read is replaced, and the replacement is recorded; the first draw uses the generator above.
HEADPIN.md:21:- **pin-committee.** At each layer the 31 destination-reading heads (the survey-final t-readers in `hp_heads.py`; L7.H7 is not among them) are re-projected so that Π is a prefill of its own and each block `[state][event]` is a tick of its own: those heads see Π plus the block, at the clock's positions. Every other head keeps ordinary causal attention over the whole history. Queries that are neither Π nor a block, on a pinned head, see only Π, with the query rotated at position `|Π|`.
HEADPIN.md:23:- **requote-256 and requote-1024.** The row-token Π, not the prose around it, is inserted again every W tokens after the first Π, up through the end of the run. Scoring reads the logit immediately before each original run token in the spliced sequence.
HEADPIN.md:35:**H1.** On the counted tapes, every tape on which pin-all is state-exact is state-exact under pin-committee, at each of 1,024, 4,096 and 8,192. This fails if any pin-all state-exact tape is not state-exact under pin-committee.
HEADPIN.md:39:**H3.** At 8,192, the number of state-exact tapes under requote-256 is strictly greater than the number under full. requote-1024 is measured and is not predicted.
HEADPIN.md:46:The smoke identity checks passed. No prediction is superseded.

One parity tape of length 1024 took 1.05 s in full and 1.207 s in pin-committee. Census max tokens 33892, sequences over the cap 0.

HEADPIN.md:107:The pin is a mask on keys, not a separate residual stream. Layers before the committee, and the heads that are not pinned, write into the hidden state the committee later reads. L7.H7 still sees the whole history. So pin-committee can fail H1 even when the mask itself is the clock's mask.
HEADPIN.md:109:The bitwise check pins every head, and that path uses the model's own grouped-query attention. The counted committee is 31 heads, re-projected as a batch of blocks and scored with the key/value heads repeated by hand. Those two kernels are not the same, and a batch of blocks is a different bf16 product from the clock's one block. The check does not certify the kernel the counted runs use.
HEADPIN.md:111:Queries outside Π and the block, on a pinned head, are defined here as attending to all of Π from position `|Π|`. The brief does not specify those positions. They matter for pin-committee because an unpinned head can later read them. They do not affect the all-heads logits, which never read those positions, so the identity check does not cover them.
HEADPIN.md:119:Two plain forwards can disagree if the attention kernel is not deterministic. The no-heads check asks them to be bitwise. If that fails while the standard-path check inside one layer also fails, the duplicate of ordinary attention is wrong and the unpinned heads in pin-committee are not ordinary attention. Qwen3-4B's config has no sliding window; the smoke record says what the loaded config actually contains. If a layer is sliding, the unpinned path must use that layer's own mask or the no-heads check cannot pass.
HEADPIN.md:124:H1 does not hold (committee state-exact wherever pin-all is: 1024: 12 of 24, 4096: 0 of 24, 8192: 0 of 24). H2 holds (pin-random state-exact counts 9, 9, 10 against full 24, 24, 22). H3 does not hold (requote-256 state-exact 0 against full 22 at 8192). H4 does not hold. For R5, neither intervention is a substitute for the clock. Pinning the destination-reading heads does not keep the tapes pin-all gets right, and a same-sized random mask is not better than leaving every head free. Putting Π back every 256 tokens does not beat the plain forward. Moving Π next to the query still raises S and both margins at delay 4096, so the table beside the query matters, but the distant medians do not fall strictly with delay on these tables.
hp_attn.py:12:  the one the identity check compares to the clock.
hp_attn.py:15:from __future__ import annotations

     1	# HEADPIN
     2	
     3	<!-- SUMMARY:START -->
     4	State-exact is the argmax of every teacher-forced state token. H1 fails. H2 passes. H3 fails. H4 fails. Counted rows 432 (the run file's grid is 432). E0 rows 32. Seed in the run file 4040. Seed in the E0 file 7071.
     5	<!-- SUMMARY:END -->
     6	
     7	## 1. Question
     8	
     9	Can Savant stay exact on a literal table while the full history stays in the sequence? The clock today is exact because every head sees only Π and the current block, and the history is deleted. This run asks two narrower versions of that, plus the margin lemma's cheapest delay test.
    10	
    11	## 2. Design
    12	
    13	Registered before any spend. State-exact means: events are supplied (the true trace is teacher-forced) and every state token's argmax, over the full vocabulary, is the true next state. A wrong read does not by itself fail a prediction. Reads are still scored, and the first error is the earlier of a wrong read and a wrong state at that step. The margin is the target logit minus the best other logit. The minimum margin of a run is the minimum over its state tokens.
    14	
    15	**Tables.** Three families, one frozen program each: parity, cups, compass. They are single-register, so the block is one (state, event). Robot is left out because it has two registers. The program is the first training draw, seed 4040, for which `literal` accepts every tape and every sequence, including requote-256, fits in 40,960 − 64 tokens. The eight tapes at each length only resample that program's events, with generator `4040|family|length|index`. Lengths 1,024, 4,096 and 8,192. A draw the clock cannot read is replaced, and the replacement is recorded; the first draw uses the generator above.
    16	
    17	**Policies.**
    18	
    19	- **pin-all.** Today's clock (`pb_pin`). The first 8 steps of each run are real ticks. If they match the certificate bitwise, the rest of the run is the certificate. If any of the 8 does not, every step of that run is a real tick. There is no read token.
    20	- **full.** One causal forward of the plain-mode sequence, the same layout as BRIDGE.
    21	- **pin-committee.** At each layer the 31 destination-reading heads (the survey-final t-readers in `hp_heads.py`; L7.H7 is not among them) are re-projected so that Π is a prefill of its own and each block `[state][event]` is a tick of its own: those heads see Π plus the block, at the clock's positions. Every other head keeps ordinary causal attention over the whole history. Queries that are neither Π nor a block, on a pinned head, see only Π, with the query rotated at position `|Π|`.
    22	- **pin-random.** The same mask on 31 other heads, drawn from the complement with seed 6060 and then sorted.
    23	- **requote-256 and requote-1024.** The row-token Π, not the prose around it, is inserted again every W tokens after the first Π, up through the end of the run. Scoring reads the logit immediately before each original run token in the spliced sequence.
    24	
    25	**E0.** Four permutation tables, 4 states and 3 events, seed 7071, from the pinned name pool. Row r is `(states[0], events[-1])`. The two programs differ only in r's destination: u is the drawn one, v is the next state in the list. The trace uses only the other events, starts at `states[1]`, and its generator does not depend on the destination or the placement. Delays 64, 256, 1,024 and 4,096 are the trace length in tokens. Distant places Π, then the trace, then the query `[s*][e*]`. Relocated places the trace, then Π, then the query. Length and token counts match within a destination. S(n) = (z_u[u] − z_u[v]) − (z_v[u] − z_v[v]), and both margins are over the full vocabulary, at the query's event token.
    26	
    27	**Identity, before any counted forward is kept.** On a short tape of the frozen parity table: pin with every head reproduces the clock's state logits bitwise, and pin with no heads reproduces a plain forward's hidden states bitwise. The counted job and the E0 job each repeat that check and stop if it fails.
    28	
    29	**Model.** Savant r3, Qwen3-4B with `/arvol/arith-s1r2/adapter_step500`, merged in memory at the start of every job. Own Modal app `polyformer-headpin`, own volume `polyformer-headpin-results`, cap $25.
    30	
    31	## 3. Predictions
    32	
    33	These four paragraphs are frozen. They are not edited after this registration. A later result may supersede one only in §3.1, with the smoke measurement that forced it, and only before a counted run.
    34	
    35	**H1.** On the counted tapes, every tape on which pin-all is state-exact is state-exact under pin-committee, at each of 1,024, 4,096 and 8,192. This fails if any pin-all state-exact tape is not state-exact under pin-committee.
    36	
    37	**H2.** At each of those lengths, the number of state-exact tapes under pin-random is less than or equal to the number under full.
    38	
    39	**H3.** At 8,192, the number of state-exact tapes under requote-256 is strictly greater than the number under full. requote-1024 is measured and is not predicted.
    40	
    41	**H4.** On the distant placement, the median of S, the median of margin_u, and the median of margin_v, each strictly decrease across delays 64, 256, 1,024, 4,096 in that order. The median of four tables is the mean of the two central values. At delay 4,096, the relocated placement's median S, median margin_u and median margin_v each exceed the distant placement's.
    42	
    43	### 3.1 After the smoke run
    44	
    45	<!-- SMOKE:START -->
    46	The smoke identity checks passed. No prediction is superseded.
    47	<!-- SMOKE:END -->

    95	| 256 | distant | 50.12 | 11.88 | 12.91 |
    96	| 256 | relocated | 76.6 | 14.81 | 16.5 |
    97	| 1024 | distant | 47.09 | 8.312 | 10.91 |
    98	| 1024 | relocated | 78.21 | 15.12 | 16.5 |
    99	| 4096 | distant | 45.32 | 10.81 | 13.12 |
   100	| 4096 | relocated | 76.8 | 14.94 | 16.19 |
   101	
   102	H4 falling: no. H4 restored at delay 4096: yes.
   103	<!-- RESULTS:END -->
   104	
   105	## 5. What could be wrong
   106	
   107	The pin is a mask on keys, not a separate residual stream. Layers before the committee, and the heads that are not pinned, write into the hidden state the committee later reads. L7.H7 still sees the whole history. So pin-committee can fail H1 even when the mask itself is the clock's mask.
   108	
   109	The bitwise check pins every head, and that path uses the model's own grouped-query attention. The counted committee is 31 heads, re-projected as a batch of blocks and scored with the key/value heads repeated by hand. Those two kernels are not the same, and a batch of blocks is a different bf16 product from the clock's one block. The check does not certify the kernel the counted runs use.
   110	
   111	Queries outside Π and the block, on a pinned head, are defined here as attending to all of Π from position `|Π|`. The brief does not specify those positions. They matter for pin-committee because an unpinned head can later read them. They do not affect the all-heads logits, which never read those positions, so the identity check does not cover them.
   112	
   113	Exactness is teacher-forced. A free run that has already stepped wrong is a different question, and this run does not ask it.
   114	
   115	These are small Part 1 tables. H1 can hold because the tables are easy, or it can hold vacuously if pin-all itself is nowhere state-exact. The results table counts how often pin-all is state-exact so that case is visible. Requote changes the length as well as the lag, so a gain can be the extra tokens rather than the lag.
   116	
   117	E0's trace never uses row r, but u and v can still occur as other rows' destinations, and the two programs differ by one token of Π. The relocation matches the multiset within a destination; it does not match the multiset across the two destinations.
   118	
   119	Two plain forwards can disagree if the attention kernel is not deterministic. The no-heads check asks them to be bitwise. If that fails while the standard-path check inside one layer also fails, the duplicate of ordinary attention is wrong and the unpinned heads in pin-committee are not ordinary attention. Qwen3-4B's config has no sliding window; the smoke record says what the loaded config actually contains. If a layer is sliding, the unpinned path must use that layer's own mask or the no-heads check cannot pass.
   120	
   121	## 6. What this means for R5
   122	
   123	<!-- R5:START -->
   124	H1 does not hold (committee state-exact wherever pin-all is: 1024: 12 of 24, 4096: 0 of 24, 8192: 0 of 24). H2 holds (pin-random state-exact counts 9, 9, 10 against full 24, 24, 22). H3 does not hold (requote-256 state-exact 0 against full 22 at 8192). H4 does not hold. For R5, neither intervention is a substitute for the clock. Pinning the destination-reading heads does not keep the tapes pin-all gets right, and a same-sized random mask is not better than leaving every head free. Putting Π back every 256 tokens does not beat the plain forward. Moving Π next to the query still raises S and both margins at delay 4096, so the table beside the query matters, but the distant medians do not fall strictly with delay on these tables.
   125	<!-- R5:END -->

     1	"""Frozen tables, tapes, and one policy's teacher-forced scores.
     2	
     3	Three single-register families (parity, cups, compass). One program is frozen per family — the first
     4	training draw, seed 4040, for which the clock can read every tape — and the eight tapes at each length
     5	only resample that program's events. Robot is not used: it has two registers, and the block in the brief
     6	is one (state, event).

     9	the first error is whichever of the two is earlier.

    44	    return C.CONTEXT - MARGIN

    47	def _dev():
    48	    import torch
    49	    return "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"

    52	def geometry(tok, lay):
    53	    """Positions of Π, of the state that enters each step, and of the event of each step."""
    54	    if lay["R"] != 1 or lay["stride"] != 2:
    55	        raise RuntimeError(f"expected one register, got R={lay['R']}")
    56	    rows = lay["rows"][0]
    57	    pi_pos = []
    58	    for row in rows:
    59	        pi_pos += [row["pos"] + j for j in range(4)]
    60	    if pi_pos != list(range(pi_pos[0], pi_pos[0] + len(pi_pos))):
    61	        raise RuntimeError("Π is not one contiguous span")
    62	    nl = C.enc(tok, "\n")
    63	    start_pos = rows[0]["pos"] - 1 - len(nl)
    64	    state_pos, event_pos = [], []
    65	    r0, st = lay["run_start"], lay["stride"]
    66	    for k in range(lay["T"]):
    67	        event_pos.append(r0 + k * st)
    68	        state_pos.append(start_pos if k == 0 else r0 + (k - 1) * st + 1)
    69	    return {"pi_pos": pi_pos, "pi_end": pi_pos[-1] + 1, "start_pos": start_pos,
    70	            "state_pos": state_pos, "event_pos": event_pos}
    71	
    72	
    73	def _check_layout(tok, lay, geo):
    74	    ids = lay["ids"]
    75	    nl = C.enc(tok, "\n")
    76	    if ids[geo["start_pos"]:geo["start_pos"] + 1 + len(nl)] != [lay["starts"][0]] + nl:
    77	        raise RuntimeError("start token is not where the geometry says")
    78	    pi = []
    79	    for row in lay["rows"][0]:
    80	        chunk = [row["s"], row["e"], row["t"]] + nl
    81	        if ids[row["pos"]:row["pos"] + 4] != chunk:
    82	            raise RuntimeError("row tokens moved")
    83	        pi += chunk
    84	    if [ids[i] for i in geo["pi_pos"]] != pi:
    85	        raise RuntimeError("Π positions do not match the row tokens")
    86	    r0, st = lay["run_start"], lay["stride"]
    87	    prev = lay["starts"][0]
    88	    for k in range(lay["T"]):
    89	        ev = ids[r0 + k * st]
    90	        wrote = ids[r0 + k * st + 1]
    91	        if ids[geo["event_pos"][k]] != ev or ids[geo["state_pos"][k]] != prev:
    92	            raise RuntimeError(f"step {k} positions disagree with the trace")
    93	        if ids[geo["event_pos"][k] + 1] != wrote:
    94	            raise RuntimeError(f"step {k} state is not the token after the event")
    95	        prev = wrote
    96	    return pi
    97	
    98	
    99	def _example(frozen, items):
   100	    a, b = frozen["span"]
   101	    q = frozen["question"][:a] + render_stream(items, frozen["fmt"]) + frozen["question"][b:]
   102	    return {"kind": "family", "prompt": chat_prompt(q), "question": q, "prog_text": frozen["prog_text"],
   103	            "program_in_prompt": False, "items": items, "L": len(items), "family": frozen["family"],
   104	            "phrasing": frozen["phrasing"], "fmt": frozen["fmt"], "answer": frozen["answer"]}
   105	
   106	
   107	def _tape_from(tok, frozen, items, L, i):
   108	    ex = _example(frozen, items)
   109	    lit, why = pb_lit.literal(ex, tok)
   110	    if lit is None:
   111	        return None, why
   112	    if len(lit["registers"]) != 1:
   113	        return None, "more than one register"
   114	    lay = C.layout(tok, ex, lit)
   115	    geo = geometry(tok, lay)
   116	    pi = _check_layout(tok, lay, geo)
   117	    return {"family": frozen["family"], "L": L, "i": i, "seed": SEED, "lay": lay, "geo": geo, "pi": pi}, None
   118	
   119	
   120	def _draw_tape(tok, frozen, L, i, redraws=5):
   121	    events = frozen["events"]
   122	    last = None
   123	    for t in range(redraws):
   124	        # t = 0 is the registered tape. A later try is only used when the clock cannot read that draw.
   125	        label = f"{SEED}|{frozen['family']}|{L}|{i}" if t == 0 else f"{SEED}|{frozen['family']}|{L}|{i}|redraw|{t}"
   126	        rng = random.Random(label)
   127	        items = [rng.choice(events) for _ in range(L)]
   128	        tape, why = _tape_from(tok, frozen, items, L, i)
   129	        if tape is not None:
   130	            tape["draw"] = t
   131	            return tape, None
   132	        last = why
   133	    return None, last
   134	
   135	
   136	def requote_of(tape, W):
   137	    lay, geo = tape["lay"], tape["geo"]
   138	    at = hp_seq.requote_positions(lay["n"], geo["pi_end"], lay["run_start"], lay["T"] * lay["stride"], W)
   139	    ids, mp = hp_seq.splice_before(lay["ids"], tape["pi"], at)
   140	    return ids, mp, at
   141	
   142	
   143	def _fits(tape):
   144	    n = tape["lay"]["n"]
   145	    out = {"full": n}
   146	    cap = limit()
   147	    over = n > cap
   148	    for W in WS:
   149	        ids, _, _ = requote_of(tape, W)
   150	        out[f"requote-{W}"] = len(ids)
   151	        over = over or len(ids) > cap
   152	    out["over"] = over
   153	    return out
   154	
   155	
   156	def _meta_of(family, attempt, inst, lit, span):
   157	    a, b, _ = span
   158	    return {"family": family, "attempt": attempt, "fmt": inst.fmt, "phrasing": inst.phrasing,
   159	            "events": list(lit["events"]), "rows": len(lit["registers"][0]["rows"]),
   160	            "prog_text": inst.program_text, "question": inst.question, "span": (a, b),
   161	            "answer": inst.answer}
   162	
   163	
   164	def _frozen(meta):
   165	    return {"family": meta["family"], "attempt": meta["attempt"], "prog_text": meta["prog_text"],
   166	            "question": meta["question"], "fmt": meta["fmt"], "phrasing": meta["phrasing"],
   167	            "span": tuple(meta["span"]), "answer": meta["answer"], "events": list(meta["events"])}
   168	
   169	
   170	def freeze_family(tok, family, lengths=LENGTHS, n=N_TAPES):
   171	    """First single-register table whose every tape the clock can read and whose requote fits."""
   172	    for attempt in range(40):
   173	        rng = random.Random(f"{SEED}|{family}|table|{attempt}")
   174	        inst = make(family, rng, L=4, split="train")
   175	        span = pb_lit.item_span(inst.question, inst.items)
   176	        if span is None:
   177	            continue
   178	        ex = {"kind": "family", "prompt": chat_prompt(inst.question), "question": inst.question,
   179	              "prog_text": inst.program_text, "program_in_prompt": False, "items": inst.items,
   180	              "L": inst.length, "family": family, "phrasing": inst.phrasing, "fmt": inst.fmt,
   181	              "answer": inst.answer}
   182	        lit, _why = pb_lit.literal(ex, tok)
   183	        if lit is None or len(lit["registers"]) != 1:
   184	            continue
   185	        meta = _meta_of(family, attempt, inst, lit, span)
   186	        frozen = _frozen(meta)
   187	        tapes, ok = [], True
   188	        for L in sorted(lengths, reverse=True):
   189	            for i in range(n):
   190	                tape, _why = _draw_tape(tok, frozen, L, i)
   191	                if tape is None or _fits(tape)["over"]:
   192	                    ok = False
   193	                    break
   194	                tapes.append(tape)
   195	            if not ok:
   196	                break
   197	        if ok:
   198	            tapes.sort(key=lambda t: (t["L"], t["i"]))
   199	            return meta, tapes
   200	    raise RuntimeError(f"no {family} table fits in {limit()} tokens")
   201	
   202	
   203	def build_corpus(tok, families=FAMILIES, lengths=LENGTHS, n=N_TAPES):
   204	    metas, tapes = [], []
   205	    for fam in families:
   206	        meta, got = freeze_family(tok, fam, lengths, n)
   207	        metas.append(meta)
   208	        tapes.extend(got)
   209	    return metas, tapes
   210	
   211	
   212	def smoke_tape(tok, meta, L=8):
   213	    """A short tape of an already frozen table. Its generator is not a counted tape's generator."""
   214	    rng = random.Random(f"{SEED}|{meta['family']}|smoke|{L}")
   215	    items = [rng.choice(list(meta["events"])) for _ in range(L)]
   216	    tape, why = _tape_from(tok, _frozen(meta), items, L, -1)
   217	    if tape is None:
   218	        raise RuntimeError(f"smoke tape: {why}")
   219	    tape["role"] = "smoke"
   220	    return tape
   221	
   222	
   223	def forward(model, ids, pin, spec):
   224	    import torch
   225	    import hp_attn
   226	    # every head pinned has to use the clock's GEMM shapes, including the residual MLP,
   227	    # which the attention wrapper cannot see
   228	    if hp_attn.is_all_pinned(spec):
   229	        pin.spec = None
   230	        with torch.no_grad():
   231	            return hp_attn.forward_all_pinned(model, ids, spec)
   232	    pin.spec = spec
   233	    try:
   234	        with torch.no_grad():
   235	            h = model.model(input_ids=torch.tensor([ids], device=_dev()), use_cache=False).last_hidden_state[0]
   236	    finally:
   237	        pin.spec = None
   238	    return h
   239	
   240	
   241	def margins_at(model, h, positions, targets, chunk=256):
   242	    import numpy as np
   243	    import torch
   244	    if not positions:
   245	        return np.zeros(0, np.float32), np.zeros(0, np.int32)
   246	    m, am = [], []
   247	    tgt = torch.as_tensor(targets)
   248	    with torch.no_grad():
   249	        for i in range(0, len(positions), chunk):
   250	            idx = torch.tensor(positions[i:i + chunk], device=h.device)
   251	            lg = model.lm_head(h[idx]).float()
   252	            tt = tgt[i:i + chunk].to(lg.device)
   253	            tl = lg.gather(1, tt[:, None])[:, 0]
   254	            other = lg.scatter(1, tt[:, None], float("-inf")).max(1).values
   255	            m.append((tl - other).cpu().numpy())
   256	            am.append(lg.argmax(1).cpu().numpy())
   257	            del lg
   258	    return np.concatenate(m).astype(np.float32), np.concatenate(am).astype(np.int32)
   259	
   260	
   261	def _pack(policy, tape, state_m, state_am, state_tgt, read_m, read_am, read_tgt, extra=None):
   262	    import numpy as np
   263	    T = len(state_tgt)
   264	    state_ok = state_am == np.asarray(state_tgt)
   265	    read_ok = read_am == np.asarray(read_tgt) if len(read_tgt) else np.ones(0, dtype=bool)
   266	    first = None
   267	    for k in range(T):
   268	        if len(read_ok) and not read_ok[k]:
   269	            first = {"step": k, "kind": "read"}
   270	            break
   271	        if not state_ok[k]:
   272	            first = {"step": k, "kind": "step"}
   273	            break
   274	    rec = {"policy": policy, "family": tape["family"], "L": tape["L"], "i": tape["i"], "seed": tape["seed"],
   275	           "n_tokens": tape["lay"]["n"], "exact_state": bool(state_ok.all()),
   276	           "exact_read": None if not len(read_ok) else bool(read_ok.all()),
   277	           "first_error": first, "min_margin": float(state_m.min()) if T else None,
   278	           "n_state_wrong": int((~state_ok).sum()), "n_read_wrong": int((~read_ok).sum()) if len(read_ok) else 0}
   279	    if extra:
   280	        rec.update(extra)
   281	    arrays = {"margin_state": state_m, "argmax_state": state_am.astype(np.int32),
   282	              "target_state": np.asarray(state_tgt, np.int32),
   283	              "margin_read": read_m, "argmax_read": read_am.astype(np.int32),
   284	              "target_read": np.asarray(read_tgt, np.int32)}
   285	    return rec, arrays
   286	
   287	
   288	def _margin_1d(lg, target):
   289	    import torch
   290	    x = lg.float()
   291	    other = x.clone()
   292	    other[int(target)] = float("-inf")
   293	    return float(x[int(target)] - other.max()), int(x.argmax())
   294	
   295	
   296	def run_pin_all(model, tape, check_first=8):
   297	    """The clock. The first `check_first` steps are real ticks; if they match the certificate bitwise,
   298	    the rest of the run is the certificate (Theorem P). Otherwise every step is a real tick."""
   299	    import numpy as np
   300	    import pb_pin
   301	    import torch
   302	    lay, geo, ids = tape["lay"], tape["geo"], tape["lay"]["ids"]
   303	    trip = [(r["s"], r["e"], r["t"]) for r in lay["rows"][0]]
   304	    cert, past, logits = pb_pin.certify(model, tape["pi"], trip, keep_logits=True)
   305	    T = lay["T"]
   306	    n_check = min(check_first, T)
   307	    bitwise = 0
   308	    sm, sa, st = [], [], []
   309	
   310	    def take(k, lg):
   311	        target = ids[geo["event_pos"][k] + 1]
   312	        margin, arg = _margin_1d(lg, target)
   313	        sm.append(margin)
   314	        sa.append(arg)
   315	        st.append(target)
   316	
   317	    for k in range(n_check):
   318	        s, e = ids[geo["state_pos"][k]], ids[geo["event_pos"][k]]
   319	        lg = pb_pin.tick(model, past, len(tape["pi"]), s, e)
   320	        bitwise += int(torch.equal(lg, logits[(s, e)]))
   321	        take(k, lg)
   322	        del lg
   323	    use_cert = bitwise == n_check
   324	    for k in range(n_check, T):
   325	        s, e = ids[geo["state_pos"][k]], ids[geo["event_pos"][k]]
   326	        lg = logits[(s, e)] if use_cert else pb_pin.tick(model, past, len(tape["pi"]), s, e)
   327	        take(k, lg)
   328	        if not use_cert:
   329	            del lg
   330	    del past, logits
   331	    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   332	    extra = {"bitwise_checks": n_check, "bitwise_equal": bitwise, "used_certificate": use_cert,
   333	             "rows": len(trip), "certified": cert["pass"]}
   334	    return _pack("pin-all", tape, np.asarray(sm, np.float32), np.asarray(sa, np.int32), st,
   335	                 np.zeros(0, np.float32), np.zeros(0, np.int32), [], extra)
   336	
   337	
   338	def _score_queries(model, h, tape, state_pos, read_pos):
   339	    ids = tape["lay"]["ids"]
   340	    geo = tape["geo"]
   341	    # state_pos / read_pos here are logit positions, already resolved in the sequence that was forwarded
   342	    state_tgt = [ids[geo["event_pos"][k] + 1] for k in range(tape["lay"]["T"])]
   343	    read_tgt = [ids[p] for p in geo["event_pos"]]
   344	    sm, sa = margins_at(model, h, state_pos, state_tgt)
   345	    rm, ra = margins_at(model, h, read_pos, read_tgt)
   346	    return sm, sa, state_tgt, rm, ra, read_tgt
   347	
   348	
   349	def run_full(model, pin, tape, policy="full"):
   350	    h = forward(model, tape["lay"]["ids"], pin, None)
   351	    geo = tape["geo"]
   352	    state_pos = geo["event_pos"]
   353	    read_pos = [p - 1 for p in geo["event_pos"]]
   354	    sm, sa, st, rm, ra, rt = _score_queries(model, h, tape, state_pos, read_pos)
   355	    del h
   356	    return _pack(policy, tape, sm, sa, st, rm, ra, rt, {"n_tokens": tape["lay"]["n"]})
   357	
   358	
   359	def run_pinned_heads(model, pin, tape, pairs, policy):
   360	    import hp_attn
   361	    spec = hp_attn.Spec(pairs, tape["geo"]["pi_pos"], tape["geo"]["state_pos"], tape["geo"]["event_pos"],
   362	                        hp_heads.N_HEADS).to(_dev())
   363	    h = forward(model, tape["lay"]["ids"], pin, spec)
   364	    geo = tape["geo"]
   365	    sm, sa, st, rm, ra, rt = _score_queries(model, h, tape, geo["event_pos"], [p - 1 for p in geo["event_pos"]])
   366	    del h
   367	    return _pack(policy, tape, sm, sa, st, rm, ra, rt)
   368	
   369	
   370	def run_requote(model, pin, tape, W):
   371	    ids, mp, at = requote_of(tape, W)
   372	    if len(ids) > limit():
   373	        raise RuntimeError(f"requote-{W} length {len(ids)} exceeds {limit()}")
   374	    h = forward(model, ids, pin, None)
   375	    geo = tape["geo"]
   376	    state_pos = [mp[geo["event_pos"][k] + 1] - 1 for k in range(tape["lay"]["T"])]
   377	    read_pos = [mp[p] - 1 for p in geo["event_pos"]]
   378	    sm, sa, st, rm, ra, rt = _score_queries(model, h, tape, state_pos, read_pos)
   379	    del h
   380	    return _pack(f"requote-{W}", tape, sm, sa, st, rm, ra, rt,
   381	                 {"n_tokens": len(ids), "n_insertions": len(at)})
   382	
   383	
   384	def run_policy(model, tok, pin, tape, policy, check_first=8):
   385	    del tok
   386	    if policy == "pin-all":
   387	        return run_pin_all(model, tape, check_first=check_first)
   388	    if policy == "full":
   389	        return run_full(model, pin, tape)
   390	    if policy == "pin-committee":
   391	        return run_pinned_heads(model, pin, tape, hp_heads.COMMITTEE, policy)
   392	    if policy == "pin-random":
   393	        return run_pinned_heads(model, pin, tape, hp_heads.RANDOM, policy)
   394	    if policy.startswith("requote-"):
   395	        return run_requote(model, pin, tape, int(policy.split("-", 1)[1]))
   396	    raise ValueError(policy)
   397	
   398	
   399	def key_of(rec):
   400	    return f"{rec['policy']}|{rec['family']}|L{rec['L']}|{rec['i']}"
   401	
   402	
   403	def e0_margin(z_t, top, top_i, target):
   404	    """Target minus the best other, from the target logit and the top two (value, index) pairs."""
   405	    if int(top_i[0]) != int(target):
   406	        return float(z_t) - float(top[0])
   407	    return float(top[0]) - float(top[1])
   408	
   409	
   410	def run_e0(model, tok, seed=E0_SEED, n_tables=E0_TABLES, delays=E0_DELAYS):
   411	    """4 perm tables, both destinations, both placements. Returns one record per (table, delay, placement)."""
   412	    import pb_pin
   413	    import torch
   414	    pool = pb_pin.name_pool(tok)
   415	    rng = random.Random(seed)
   416	    tables = [pb_pin.draw_table(rng, pool["train"], n=4, a=3, shape="perm") for _ in range(n_tables)]
   417	    rows = []
   418	    for ti, table in enumerate(tables):
   419	        for delay in delays:
   420	            built = hp_e0.sequences(table, pool["id_of"], pool["nl"], delay, f"{seed}|t{ti}")
   421	            if built["trace_len"] != delay:
   422	                raise RuntimeError("delay is not the trace length")
   423	            got, n_tokens = {}, {}
   424	            for (name, reloc), seq in built["seqs"].items():
   425	                ids = seq["ids"]
   426	                if len(ids) > limit():
   427	                    raise RuntimeError(f"E0 length {len(ids)} exceeds {limit()}")
   428	                with torch.no_grad():
   429	                    h = model.model(input_ids=torch.tensor([ids], device=_dev()),
   430	                                    use_cache=False).last_hidden_state[0]
   431	                    lg = model.lm_head(h[-1:]).float()[0]
   432	                topv, topi = torch.topk(lg, 2)
   433	                got[(name, reloc)] = {"z_u": float(lg[built["u_id"]]), "z_v": float(lg[built["v_id"]]),
   434	                                      "top": [float(topv[0]), float(topv[1])],
   435	                                      "top_i": [int(topi[0]), int(topi[1])]}
   436	                n_tokens[(name, reloc)] = len(ids)
   437	                del h, lg
   438	            for reloc in (False, True):
   439	                if n_tokens[("u", reloc)] != n_tokens[("v", reloc)]:
   440	                    raise RuntimeError("u and v programs differ in length")
   441	                if n_tokens[("u", False)] != n_tokens[("u", True)]:
   442	                    raise RuntimeError("relocation changed the length")
   443	                u, v = got[("u", reloc)], got[("v", reloc)]
   444	                zuu, zuv, zvu, zvv = u["z_u"], u["z_v"], v["z_u"], v["z_v"]
   445	                rows.append({
   446	                    "table": ti, "delay": int(delay), "relocate": bool(reloc), "seed": seed,
   447	                    "S": hp_seq.s_value(zuu, zuv, zvu, zvv),
   448	                    "margin_u": e0_margin(zuu, u["top"], u["top_i"], built["u_id"]),
   449	                    "margin_v": e0_margin(zvv, v["top"], v["top_i"], built["v_id"]),
   450	                    "z_uu": zuu, "z_uv": zuv, "z_vu": zvu, "z_vv": zvv,
   451	                    "top_u": u["top"], "top_u_i": u["top_i"], "top_v": v["top"], "top_v_i": v["top_i"],
   452	                    "u_id": built["u_id"], "v_id": built["v_id"],
   453	                    "s": built["s"], "e": built["e"], "u": built["u"], "v": built["v"],
   454	                    "n_tokens": n_tokens[("u", reloc)], "n_states": len(table["states"]),
   455	                    "n_events": len(table["events"]), "shape": table["shape"],
   456	                })
   457	    return rows

    21	def atomic(path, obj):
    22	    path = pathlib.Path(path)
    23	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    24	    tmp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".tmp")
    25	    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(obj, indent=1, default=str))

    29	def check_adapter(path):
    30	    p = pathlib.Path(path)
    31	    if p.exists():
    32	        return
    33	    parent = p.parent
    34	    names = sorted(x.name for x in parent.iterdir()) if parent.exists() else []
    35	    raise SystemExit(f"adapter directory missing; entries beside it: {names}")

    38	def json_meta(meta):
    39	    keep = ("family", "attempt", "fmt", "phrasing", "events", "rows", "prog_text", "question", "answer")
    40	    out = {k: meta[k] for k in keep}

    45	def census(tapes):
    46	    rows, over = [], []
    47	    for tape in tapes:
    48	        fit = H._fits(tape)
    49	        row = {"family": tape["family"], "L": tape["L"], "i": tape["i"], "draw": tape.get("draw", 0)}
    50	        row.update({k: v for k, v in fit.items() if k != "over"})
    51	        rows.append(row)
    52	        if fit["over"]:
    53	            over.append({"family": tape["family"], "L": tape["L"], "i": tape["i"]})
    54	    peak = 0
    55	    for row in rows:
    56	        peak = max(peak, row["full"], row["requote-256"], row["requote-1024"])
    57	    return {"cap": H.limit(), "n": len(rows), "over": over, "max_tokens": peak, "rows": rows}

    59	
    60	def _hook_first(attn):
    61	    store, orig = {}, attn.forward
    62	
    63	    def wrapped(hidden_states, position_embeddings, attention_mask, past_key_values=None, **kw):
    64	        if "h" not in store:
    65	            store["h"] = hidden_states.detach().clone()
    66	            store["mask"] = None if attention_mask is None else attention_mask.detach().clone()
    67	        out = orig(hidden_states=hidden_states, position_embeddings=position_embeddings,
    68	                   attention_mask=attention_mask, past_key_values=past_key_values, **kw)
    69	        if "y" not in store:
    70	            store["y"] = out[0].detach().clone()
    71	        return out
    72	
    73	    attn.forward = wrapped
    74	    return store, orig
    75	
    76	
    77	def _restore(attn, orig):
    78	    attn.forward = orig
    79	
    80	
    81	def _max_abs(a, b):
    82	    import torch
    83	    return float((a.float() - b.float()).abs().max())

    86	def identity(model, tok, pin, tape):
    87	    """All heads must match the clock bitwise. No heads must match a plain forward bitwise."""
    88	    import torch
    89	    import pb_pin
    90	    pin.spec = None
    91	    ids, geo, pi = tape["lay"]["ids"], tape["geo"], tape["pi"]
    92	    P, T = len(pi), tape["lay"]["T"]
    93	    dev = H._dev()
    94	    layer0 = model.model.layers[0].self_attn
    95	
    96	    store, orig = _hook_first(layer0)
    97	    try:
    98	        past = pb_pin.prefill(model, pi)
    99	    finally:
   100	        _restore(layer0, orig)
   101	    prefill_mask = store.get("mask", "missing")
   102	
   103	    # every layer's block input on the first tick, and every tick's final hidden state
   104	    tick_layers, hooks = {}, []
   105	    for layer in model.model.layers:
   106	        attn = layer.self_attn
   107	        box, orig = {}, attn.forward
   108	
   109	        def wrapped(hidden_states, position_embeddings, attention_mask, past_key_values=None, _attn=attn, _orig=orig, _box=box, **kw):
   110	            if "h" not in _box:
   111	                _box["h"] = hidden_states.detach().clone()
   112	                _box["mask"] = None if attention_mask is None else attention_mask.detach().clone()
   113	            out = _orig(hidden_states=hidden_states, position_embeddings=position_embeddings,
   114	                        attention_mask=attention_mask, past_key_values=past_key_values, **kw)
   115	            if "y" not in _box:
   116	                _box["y"] = out[0].detach().clone()
   117	            return out
   118	
   119	        attn.forward = wrapped
   120	        hooks.append((attn, orig, box))
   121	    norm = model.model.norm
   122	    norm_orig, finals = norm.forward, []
   123	
   124	    def norm_wrapped(x, _orig=norm_orig):
   125	        y = _orig(x)
   126	        finals.append(y.detach().clone())
   127	        return y
   128	
   129	    norm.forward = norm_wrapped
   130	    ticks = []
   131	    try:
   132	        for k in range(T):
   133	            s, e = ids[geo["state_pos"][k]], ids[geo["event_pos"][k]]
   134	            ticks.append(pb_pin.tick(model, past, P, s, e).detach().clone())
   135	    finally:
   136	        norm.forward = norm_orig
   137	        for attn, orig, box in hooks:
   138	            attn.forward = orig
   139	            tick_layers[attn.layer_idx] = box.get("h")
   140	    layer0_box = next(box for attn, _orig, box in hooks if attn.layer_idx == 0)
   141	    tick_h, tick_mask, tick_y = layer0_box.get("h"), layer0_box.get("mask"), layer0_box.get("y")
   142	    del past
   143	
   144	    spec = hp_attn.Spec(hp_heads.all_heads(), geo["pi_pos"], geo["state_pos"], geo["event_pos"],
   145	                        hp_heads.N_HEADS, debug=True).to(dev)
   146	    h = H.forward(model, ids, pin, spec)
   147	    # the clock's logit is lm_head of the two block positions together; a different batch is a different bf16 product
   148	    step_h_abs, step_logit_abs = [], []
   149	    hidden_equal, logit_equal = True, True
   150	    with torch.no_grad():
   151	        for k in range(T):
   152	            ref = finals[k][0, -1]
   153	            got_h = h[geo["event_pos"][k]]
   154	            hidden_equal = hidden_equal and bool(torch.equal(got_h, ref))
   155	            step_h_abs.append(_max_abs(got_h, ref))
   156	            pair = torch.stack([h[geo["state_pos"][k]], got_h])[None]
   157	            lg = model.lm_head(pair)[0, -1]
   158	            logit_equal = logit_equal and bool(torch.equal(lg, ticks[k]))
   159	            step_logit_abs.append(_max_abs(lg, ticks[k]))
   160	    logit_abs = max(step_logit_abs) if step_logit_abs else None
   161	    long_h = None if spec.debug is None else spec.debug.get("long_h0")
   162	    input_equal = long_h is not None and tick_h is not None and torch.equal(long_h, tick_h)
   163	    input_abs = None if long_h is None or tick_h is None else _max_abs(long_h, tick_h)
   164	    block = None if spec.debug is None else spec.debug.get("long_block0")
   165	    y_direct = None if spec.debug is None else spec.debug.get("layer0_y")
   166	    block_equal, block_abs = False, None
   167	    if y_direct is not None and tick_y is not None:
   168	        block_equal = bool(torch.equal(y_direct, tick_y))
   169	        block_abs = _max_abs(y_direct, tick_y)
   170	    elif block is not None and tick_y is not None:
   171	        custom_y = layer0.o_proj(block.reshape(1, 2, -1).contiguous())
   172	        block_equal = bool(torch.equal(custom_y, tick_y))
   173	        block_abs = _max_abs(custom_y, tick_y)
   174	    ours = hp_attn.clock_mask(torch, P, dev)
   175	    mask_equal = tick_mask is not None and torch.equal(ours, tick_mask)
   176	    mask_abs = None
   177	    if tick_mask is not None and tick_mask.shape == ours.shape and tick_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
   178	        mask_abs = int((ours != tick_mask).sum())
   179	
   180	    h_full = H.forward(model, ids, pin, None)
   181	    spec0 = hp_attn.Spec([], geo["pi_pos"], geo["state_pos"], geo["event_pos"], hp_heads.N_HEADS).to(dev)
   182	    h_none = H.forward(model, ids, pin, spec0)
   183	    none_equal = bool(torch.equal(h_full, h_none))
   184	    none_abs = _max_abs(h_full, h_none)
   185	
   186	    spec_std = hp_attn.Spec([], geo["pi_pos"], geo["state_pos"], geo["event_pos"], hp_heads.N_HEADS,
   187	                            debug=True, std_only=True).to(dev)
   188	    H.forward(model, ids, pin, spec_std)
   189	    std_equal = bool(spec_std.debug and spec_std.debug.get("std_equal"))
   190	    std_max = None if spec_std.debug is None else spec_std.debug.get("std_max")
   191	
   192	    cfg = model.config
   193	    layer_types = list(getattr(cfg, "layer_types", []) or [])
   194	    prefill_none = prefill_mask is None
   195	    block_h = {} if spec.debug is None else spec.debug.get("block_h", {})
   196	    layer_abs, first_layer = [], None
   197	    for i in range(int(cfg.num_hidden_layers)):
   198	        a, b = block_h.get(i), tick_layers.get(i)
   199	        if a is None or b is None:
   200	            layer_abs.append(None)
   201	            continue
   202	        diff = _max_abs(a, b)
   203	        layer_abs.append(diff)
   204	        if diff != 0.0 and first_layer is None:
   205	            first_layer = i
   206	    passed = bool(logit_equal and hidden_equal and input_equal and block_equal and mask_equal and none_equal
   207	                  and std_equal and prefill_none)
   208	    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   209	    return {"pass": passed, "T": T, "P": P, "n_tokens": len(ids),
   210	            "all_heads_logits_equal": bool(logit_equal), "all_heads_max_abs": logit_abs,
   211	            "all_heads_hidden_equal": bool(hidden_equal), "step_hidden_max_abs": step_h_abs,
   212	            "step_logit_max_abs": step_logit_abs, "first_layer_diff": first_layer, "layer_input_max_abs": layer_abs,
   213	            "layer0_input_equal": bool(input_equal), "layer0_input_max_abs": input_abs,
   214	            "layer0_block_equal": bool(block_equal), "layer0_block_max_abs": block_abs,
   215	            "mask_equal": bool(mask_equal), "mask_mismatches": mask_abs,
   216	            "prefill_mask_none": bool(prefill_none),
   217	            "no_heads_hidden_equal": bool(none_equal), "no_heads_max_abs": none_abs,
   218	            "std_equal": std_equal, "std_max": std_max,
   219	            "n_layers": int(cfg.num_hidden_layers), "n_heads": int(cfg.num_attention_heads),
   220	            "n_kv": int(cfg.num_key_value_heads),
   221	            "layer_types": sorted(set(layer_types)),
   222	            "use_sliding_window": bool(getattr(cfg, "use_sliding_window", False)),
   223	            "tick_mask_shape": None if tick_mask is None else list(tick_mask.shape),
   224	            "tick_mask_dtype": None if tick_mask is None else str(tick_mask.dtype),
   225	            "all_heads_path": None if spec.debug is None else spec.debug.get("path")}
   226

   228	def _done(path):
   229	    got = set()
   230	    if path.exists():
   231	        for line in path.read_text().splitlines():
   232	            if line.strip():
   233	                got.add(H.key_of(json.loads(line)))
   234	    return got

   243	def recount_runs(out):
   244	    """Second pass over the npz arrays. Returns mismatches; does not trust the json flags alone."""
   245	    import numpy as np
   246	    rows = []
   247	    path = pathlib.Path(out) / "runs.jsonl"
   248	    if not path.exists():
   249	        return ["no runs.jsonl"]
   250	    bad = []
   251	    for line in path.read_text().splitlines():
   252	        if not line.strip():
   253	            continue
   254	        rec = json.loads(line)
   255	        key = H.key_of(rec)
   256	        npz = np.load(pathlib.Path(out) / "runs" / f"{key.replace('|', '_')}.npz")
   257	        state_ok = npz["argmax_state"] == npz["target_state"]
   258	        exact = bool(state_ok.all())
   259	        margin = float(npz["margin_state"].min()) if len(npz["margin_state"]) else None
   260	        if exact != rec["exact_state"] or abs(margin - rec["min_margin"]) > 1e-3:
   261	            bad.append(key)
   262	        first = None
   263	        read_ok = npz["argmax_read"] == npz["target_read"]
   264	        for k in range(len(state_ok)):
   265	            if len(read_ok) and not read_ok[k]:
   266	                first = {"step": k, "kind": "read"}
   267	                break
   268	            if not state_ok[k]:
   269	                first = {"step": k, "kind": "step"}
   270	                break
   271	        if first != rec["first_error"]:
   272	            bad.append(key + ":first")
   273	        rows.append(exact)
   274	    return bad
   275	
   276	
   277	def recount_e0(path):
   278	    bad = []
   279	    if not pathlib.Path(path).exists():
   280	        return ["no e0 jsonl"]
   281	    for line in pathlib.Path(path).read_text().splitlines():
   282	        if not line.strip():
   283	            continue
   284	        r = json.loads(line)
   285	        s = hp_seq.s_value(r["z_uu"], r["z_uv"], r["z_vu"], r["z_vv"])
   286	        mu = H.e0_margin(r["z_uu"], r["top_u"], r["top_u_i"], r["u_id"])
   287	        mv = H.e0_margin(r["z_vv"], r["top_v"], r["top_v_i"], r["v_id"])
   288	        if abs(s - r["S"]) > 1e-4 or abs(mu - r["margin_u"]) > 1e-4 or abs(mv - r["margin_v"]) > 1e-4:
   289	            bad.append(f"t{r['table']}-d{r['delay']}-{r['relocate']}")
   290	    return bad
   291	
   292	
   293	def _load(adapter):
   294	    import br_core as C
   295	    check_adapter(adapter)
   296	    t0 = time.time()
   297	    tok = C.tokenizer()
   298	    model = C.load_model(adapter)
   299	    if model.config.num_hidden_layers != hp_heads.N_LAYERS or model.config.num_attention_heads != hp_heads.N_HEADS:
   300	        raise SystemExit("model head count does not match the committee table")
   301	    log(loaded=round(time.time() - t0, 1))
   302	    return tok, model, C.host()
   303	
   304	
   305	def mode_smoke(a):
   306	    import br_core as C
   307	    hp_heads.check()
   308	    check_adapter(a.adapter)
   309	    tok = C.tokenizer()
   310	    t0 = time.time()
   311	    metas, tapes = H.build_corpus(tok)
   312	    cens = census(tapes)
   313	    cens["tables"] = [json_meta(m) for m in metas]
   314	    cens["seconds"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)
   315	    atomic(pathlib.Path(a.out) / "census.json", cens)
   316	    log(census_n=cens["n"], max_tokens=cens["max_tokens"], over=len(cens["over"]), seconds=cens["seconds"])
   317	    if cens["over"]:
   318	        raise SystemExit(3)
   319	    timing_tape = next(t for t in tapes if t["family"] == "parity" and t["L"] == 1024 and t["i"] == 0)
   320	    meta_p = next(m for m in metas if m["family"] == "parity")
   321	    del tapes
   322	    tok, model, host = _load(a.adapter)
   323	    # tokenizer() was already called; _load calls it again. That is cheap. The first tok is the same cache.
   324	    pin = hp_attn.Pin(model)
   325	    tape = H.smoke_tape(tok, meta_p, 8)
   326	    ident = identity(model, tok, pin, tape)
   327	    atomic(pathlib.Path(a.out) / "identity.json", ident)
   328	    log(identity_pass=ident["pass"], logits=ident["all_heads_logits_equal"], none=ident["no_heads_hidden_equal"],
   329	        std=ident["std_equal"], mask=ident["mask_equal"])
   330	    if not ident["pass"]:
   331	        raise SystemExit(2)
   332	    t1 = time.perf_counter()
   333	    H.run_policy(model, tok, pin, timing_tape, "full")
   334	    full_s = round(time.perf_counter() - t1, 3)
   335	    t1 = time.perf_counter()
   336	    H.run_policy(model, tok, pin, timing_tape, "pin-committee")
   337	    comm_s = round(time.perf_counter() - t1, 3)
   338	    atomic(pathlib.Path(a.out) / "smoke.json",
   339	           {"host": host, "adapter": a.adapter, "identity": ident, "census_max_tokens": cens["max_tokens"],
   340	            "census_n": cens["n"], "census_over": len(cens["over"]),
   341	            "time_full_1024": full_s, "time_pin_committee_1024": comm_s,
   342	            "tables": [{k: m[k] for k in ("family", "attempt", "fmt", "phrasing", "rows")} for m in metas]})
   343	    log(time_full_1024=full_s, time_pin_committee_1024=comm_s)
   344	
   345	
   346	def _recheck(model, tok, pin, meta, out):
   347	    tape = H.smoke_tape(tok, meta, 8)
   348	    ident = identity(model, tok, pin, tape)
   349	    atomic(pathlib.Path(out) / "identity.json", ident)
   350	    log(identity_pass=ident["pass"])
   351	    if not ident["pass"]:
   352	        raise SystemExit(2)
   353	
   354	
   355	def mode_count(a):
   356	    import br_core as C
   357	    hp_heads.check()
   358	    check_adapter(a.adapter)
   359	    families = tuple(a.families.split(","))
   360	    lengths = tuple(int(x) for x in a.lengths.split(","))
   361	    policies = tuple(a.policies.split(","))
   362	    out = pathlib.Path(a.resume) if a.resume else pathlib.Path(a.out)
   363	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   364	    t0 = time.time()
   365	    tok = C.tokenizer()
   366	    metas, tapes = H.build_corpus(tok, families, lengths, a.n)
   367	    cens = census(tapes)
   368	    atomic(out / "census.json", {**cens, "tables": [json_meta(m) for m in metas]})
   369	    log(corpus=len(tapes), max_tokens=cens["max_tokens"], over=len(cens["over"]), seconds=round(time.time() - t0, 1))
   370	    if cens["over"]:
   371	        raise SystemExit(3)
   372	    tok, model, host = _load(a.adapter)
   373	    pin = hp_attn.Pin(model)
   374	    meta_p = next(m for m in metas if m["family"] == "parity")
   375	    _recheck(model, tok, pin, meta_p, out)
   376	    atomic(out / "run.json",
   377	           {"host": host, "seed": H.SEED, "families": list(families), "lengths": list(lengths),
   378	            "n": a.n, "policies": list(policies), "adapter": a.adapter, "status": "running"})
   379	    (out / "runs").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
   380	    done = _done(out / "runs.jsonl")
   381	    import numpy as np
   382	    n_done = 0
   383	    for policy in policies:
   384	        for tape in tapes:
   385	            rec_key = f"{policy}|{tape['family']}|L{tape['L']}|{tape['i']}"
   386	            if rec_key in done:
   387	                continue
   388	            t1 = time.perf_counter()
   389	            rec, arrays = H.run_policy(model, tok, pin, tape, policy, check_first=a.check_first)
   390	            np.savez_compressed(out / "runs" / f"{rec_key.replace('|', '_')}.npz", **arrays)
   391	            _append(out / "runs.jsonl", rec)
   392	            n_done += 1
   393	            log(done=rec_key, exact_state=rec["exact_state"], first=rec["first_error"],
   394	                seconds=round(time.perf_counter() - t1, 1))
   395	    bad = recount_runs(out)
   396	    atomic(out / "recount.json", {"bad": bad, "n_lines": sum(1 for ln in (out / "runs.jsonl").read_text().splitlines() if ln.strip())})
   397	    if bad:
   398	        log(recount_bad=len(bad))
   399	        raise SystemExit(4)
   400	    run = json.loads((out / "run.json").read_text())
   401	    run["status"] = "complete"
   402	    run["seconds"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)
   403	    atomic(out / "run.json", run)
   404	    log(finished=run["seconds"], wrote=n_done)
   405	
   406	
   407	def mode_e0(a):
   408	    import br_core as C
   409	    hp_heads.check()
   410	    check_adapter(a.adapter)
   411	    tok = C.tokenizer()
   412	    # a short program, only to re-check the mechanism before any E0 forward is kept
   413	    meta, _tapes = H.freeze_family(tok, "parity", lengths=(8,), n=1)
   414	    tok, model, host = _load(a.adapter)
   415	    pin = hp_attn.Pin(model)
   416	    _recheck(model, tok, pin, meta, a.out)
   417	    t0 = time.time()
   418	    rows = H.run_e0(model, tok)
   419	    path = pathlib.Path(a.out) / "e0.jsonl"
   420	    path.write_text("".join(json.dumps(r) + "\n" for r in rows))
   421	    bad = recount_e0(path)
   422	    atomic(pathlib.Path(a.out) / "e0_meta.json",
   423	           {"host": host, "seed": H.E0_SEED, "n": len(rows), "bad": bad, "adapter": a.adapter,
   424	            "seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 1)})
   425	    if bad:
   426	        raise SystemExit(4)
   427	    log(e0=len(rows), seconds=round(time.time() - t0, 1))
   428	
   429	
   430	def main():
   431	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   432	    ap.add_argument("--mode", required=True, choices=("smoke", "count", "e0"))
   433	    ap.add_argument("--adapter", default="/arvol/arith-s1r2/adapter_step500")
   434	    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
   435	    ap.add_argument("--families", default=",".join(H.FAMILIES))
   436	    ap.add_argument("--lengths", default=",".join(str(x) for x in H.LENGTHS))
   437	    ap.add_argument("--n", type=int, default=H.N_TAPES)
   438	    ap.add_argument("--policies", default=",".join(H.POLICIES))
   439	    ap.add_argument("--check_first", type=int, default=8)
   440	    ap.add_argument("--resume", default="")
   441	    a = ap.parse_args()
   442	    pathlib.Path(a.out).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   443	    {"smoke": mode_smoke, "count": mode_count, "e0": mode_e0}[a.mode](a)
   444	
   445	
   446	if __name__ == "__main__":
   447	    try:
   448	        main()
   449	    except SystemExit:
   450	        raise
   451	    except Exception as exc:
   452	        log(error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
   453	        raise
     1	"""Fill the result markers in HEADPIN.md from the result files. Two recounts; it writes nothing if they disagree.
     2	
     3	Every number in the markers comes from a file. Design text outside the markers is left alone, including the
     4	registered predictions.
     5	"""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	import json
    10	import pathlib
    11	import statistics
    12	import sys
    13	
    14	HERE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    15	sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
    16	
    17	import hp_core as H  # noqa: E402
    18	import hp_seq  # noqa: E402
    19	
    20	LENGTHS = (1024, 4096, 8192)
    21	POLICIES = H.POLICIES
    22	
    23	
    24	def _load_jsonl(path):
    25	    rows = []
    26	    for line in pathlib.Path(path).read_text().splitlines():
    27	        if line.strip():
    28	            rows.append(json.loads(line))
    29	    return rows
    30	
    31	
    32	def recount_runs(out):
    33	    """Way A is the json flags. Way B recomputes them from the npz. Both are returned; the caller checks equality."""
    34	    import numpy as np
    35	    out = pathlib.Path(out)
    36	    way_a, way_b = [], []
    37	    for rec in _load_jsonl(out / "runs.jsonl"):
    38	        key = H.key_of(rec)
    39	        z = np.load(out / "runs" / f"{key.replace('|', '_')}.npz")
    40	        state_ok = z["argmax_state"] == z["target_state"]
    41	        read_ok = z["argmax_read"] == z["target_read"]
    42	        first = None
    43	        for k in range(len(state_ok)):
    44	            if len(read_ok) and not bool(read_ok[k]):
    45	                first = {"step": int(k), "kind": "read"}
    46	                break
    47	            if not bool(state_ok[k]):
    48	                first = {"step": int(k), "kind": "step"}
    49	                break
    50	        b = {"key": key, "policy": rec["policy"], "family": rec["family"], "L": int(rec["L"]), "i": int(rec["i"]),
    51	             "exact_state": bool(state_ok.all()),
    52	             "exact_read": None if len(read_ok) == 0 else bool(read_ok.all()),
    53	             "min_margin": float(z["margin_state"].min()),
    54	             "first_error": first,
    55	             "n_state_wrong": int((~state_ok).sum()),
    56	             "n_read_wrong": int((~read_ok).sum()) if len(read_ok) else 0}
    57	        a = {"key": key, "policy": rec["policy"], "family": rec["family"], "L": int(rec["L"]), "i": int(rec["i"]),
    58	             "exact_state": bool(rec["exact_state"]), "exact_read": rec["exact_read"],
    59	             "min_margin": float(rec["min_margin"]), "first_error": rec["first_error"],
    60	             "n_state_wrong": int(rec["n_state_wrong"]), "n_read_wrong": int(rec["n_read_wrong"])}
    61	        way_a.append(a)
    62	        way_b.append(b)
    63	    return way_a, way_b
    64	
    65	
    66	def _same_run(a, b):
    67	    if a["exact_state"] != b["exact_state"] or a["exact_read"] != b["exact_read"]:
    68	        return False
    69	    if a["first_error"] != b["first_error"]:
    70	        return False
    71	    if abs(a["min_margin"] - b["min_margin"]) > 1e-3:
    72	        return False
    73	    if a["n_state_wrong"] != b["n_state_wrong"] or a["n_read_wrong"] != b["n_read_wrong"]:
    74	        return False
    75	    return True
    76	
    77	
    78	def recount_e0(path):
    79	    way_a, way_b = [], []
    80	    for r in _load_jsonl(path):
    81	        s = hp_seq.s_value(r["z_uu"], r["z_uv"], r["z_vu"], r["z_vv"])
    82	        mu = H.e0_margin(r["z_uu"], r["top_u"], r["top_u_i"], r["u_id"])
    83	        mv = H.e0_margin(r["z_vv"], r["top_v"], r["top_v_i"], r["v_id"])
    84	        b = {"table": r["table"], "delay": r["delay"], "relocate": r["relocate"],
    85	             "S": s, "margin_u": mu, "margin_v": mv}
    86	        a = {"table": r["table"], "delay": r["delay"], "relocate": r["relocate"],
    87	             "S": r["S"], "margin_u": r["margin_u"], "margin_v": r["margin_v"]}
    88	        way_a.append(a)
    89	        way_b.append(b)
    90	    return way_a, way_b
    91	
    92	
    93	def _same_e0(a, b):
    94	    return (abs(a["S"] - b["S"]) <= 1e-4 and abs(a["margin_u"] - b["margin_u"]) <= 1e-4
    95	            and abs(a["margin_v"] - b["margin_v"]) <= 1e-4)
    96	
    97	
    98	def _cell(rows, policy, L):
    99	    return [r for r in rows if r["policy"] == policy and r["L"] == L]
   100	
   101	
   102	def _fmt(x):
   103	    if x is None:
   104	        return "—"
   105	    if isinstance(x, bool):
   106	        return "yes" if x else "no"
   107	    if isinstance(x, int):
   108	        return str(x)
   109	    return f"{x:.4g}"
   110	
   111	
   112	def _median(xs):
   113	    return statistics.median(xs)
   114	
   115	
   116	def verdicts(rows, e0):
   117	    out = {}
   118	    # H1: every pin-all state-exact tape is pin-committee state-exact, at each length
   119	    h1 = {}
   120	    for L in LENGTHS:
   121	        pin = {(r["family"], r["i"]): r["exact_state"] for r in _cell(rows, "pin-all", L)}
   122	        com = {(r["family"], r["i"]): r["exact_state"] for r in _cell(rows, "pin-committee", L)}
   123	        both = [k for k in pin if k in com]
   124	        holds = all(com[k] for k in both if pin[k]) if both else False
   125	        h1[L] = {"holds": holds, "n": len(both), "pin_all_exact": sum(pin[k] for k in both),
   126	                 "committee_where_pin": sum(com[k] for k in both if pin[k])}
   127	    out["H1"] = h1
   128	    out["H1_pass"] = all(h1[L]["holds"] and h1[L]["n"] > 0 for L in LENGTHS)
   129	    h2 = {}
   130	    for L in LENGTHS:
   131	        rnd = sum(r["exact_state"] for r in _cell(rows, "pin-random", L))
   132	        full = sum(r["exact_state"] for r in _cell(rows, "full", L))
   133	        n = len(_cell(rows, "pin-random", L))
   134	        h2[L] = {"holds": n > 0 and len(_cell(rows, "full", L)) == n and rnd <= full,
   135	                 "random": rnd, "full": full, "n": n}
   136	    out["H2"] = h2
   137	    out["H2_pass"] = all(h2[L]["holds"] for L in LENGTHS)
   138	    r256 = sum(r["exact_state"] for r in _cell(rows, "requote-256", 8192))
   139	    full8 = sum(r["exact_state"] for r in _cell(rows, "full", 8192))
   140	    n8 = len(_cell(rows, "requote-256", 8192))
   141	    out["H3"] = {"holds": n8 > 0 and len(_cell(rows, "full", 8192)) == n8 and r256 > full8,
   142	                 "requote": r256, "full": full8, "n": n8}
   143	    out["H3_pass"] = out["H3"]["holds"]
   144	    out["H4"] = _h4(e0)
   145	    out["H4_pass"] = out["H4"]["pass"]
   146	    return out
   147	
   148	
   149	def _h4(e0):
   150	    delays = list(H.E0_DELAYS)
   151	    far, near = {}, {}
   152	    for delay in delays:
   153	        f = [r for r in e0 if r["delay"] == delay and r["relocate"] is False]
   154	        n = [r for r in e0 if r["delay"] == delay and r["relocate"] is True]
   155	        if len(f) != H.E0_TABLES or len(n) != H.E0_TABLES:
   156	            return {"pass": False, "reason": "incomplete", "far": {}, "near": {}}
   157	        far[delay] = {k: _median([r[k] for r in f]) for k in ("S", "margin_u", "margin_v")}
   158	        near[delay] = {k: _median([r[k] for r in n]) for k in ("S", "margin_u", "margin_v")}
   159	    falling = True
   160	    for key in ("S", "margin_u", "margin_v"):
   161	        seq = [far[d][key] for d in delays]
   162	        if not all(seq[i] > seq[i + 1] for i in range(len(seq) - 1)):
   163	            falling = False
   164	    d = 4096
   165	    restored = all(near[d][k] > far[d][k] for k in ("S", "margin_u", "margin_v"))
   166	    return {"pass": falling and restored, "falling": falling, "restored": restored, "far": far, "near": near}
   167	
   168	
   169	def _table(rows):
   170	    lines = ["| policy | length | n | state-exact | read-exact | first read | first step | earliest step | median min margin |",
   171	             "| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |"]
   172	    for policy in POLICIES:
   173	        for L in LENGTHS:
   174	            cell = _cell(rows, policy, L)
   175	            if not cell:
   176	                lines.append(f"| {policy} | {L} | 0 |  |  |  |  |  |")
   177	                continue
   178	            n = len(cell)
   179	            se = sum(r["exact_state"] for r in cell)
   180	            reads = [r["exact_read"] for r in cell if r["exact_read"] is not None]
   181	            re = "—" if not reads else str(sum(reads))
   182	            fr = sum(1 for r in cell if r["first_error"] and r["first_error"]["kind"] == "read")
   183	            fs = sum(1 for r in cell if r["first_error"] and r["first_error"]["kind"] == "step")
   184	            steps = [r["first_error"]["step"] for r in cell if r["first_error"]]
   185	            early = min(steps) if steps else None
   186	            med = _median([r["min_margin"] for r in cell])
   187	            lines.append(f"| {policy} | {L} | {n} | {se} | {re} | {fr} | {fs} | {_fmt(early)} | {_fmt(med)} |")
   188	    return "\n".join(lines)
   189	
   190	
   191	def _e0_table(h4):
   192	    if not h4.get("far"):
   193	        return "E0 medians are not in the files."
   194	    lines = ["| delay | placement | median S | median margin u | median margin v |",
   195	             "| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |"]
   196	    for delay in H.E0_DELAYS:
   197	        for label, bucket in (("distant", h4["far"]), ("relocated", h4["near"])):
   198	            m = bucket[delay]
   199	            lines.append(f"| {delay} | {label} | {_fmt(m['S'])} | {_fmt(m['margin_u'])} | {_fmt(m['margin_v'])} |")
   200	    return "\n".join(lines)
   201	
   202	
   203	def _r5(v):
   204	    h1 = "holds" if v["H1_pass"] else "does not hold"
   205	    h2 = "holds" if v["H2_pass"] else "does not hold"
   206	    h3 = "holds" if v["H3_pass"] else "does not hold"
   207	    h4 = "holds" if v["H4_pass"] else "does not hold"
   208	    ante = ", ".join(f"{L}: {v['H1'][L]['committee_where_pin']} of {v['H1'][L]['pin_all_exact']}" for L in LENGTHS)
   209	    opening = (
   210	        f"H1 {h1} (committee state-exact wherever pin-all is: {ante}). "
   211	        f"H2 {h2} (pin-random state-exact counts {', '.join(str(v['H2'][L]['random']) for L in LENGTHS)} "
   212	        f"against full {', '.join(str(v['H2'][L]['full']) for L in LENGTHS)}). "
   213	        f"H3 {h3} (requote-256 state-exact {v['H3']['requote']} against full {v['H3']['full']} at 8192). "
   214	        f"H4 {h4}."
   215	    )
   216	    if v["H1_pass"] and v["H3_pass"]:
   217	        mechanism = (
   218	            "For R5, both the head pin and the 256-token requote kept state-exactness with the history still "
   219	            "in the sequence, so the next design can keep the conversation and still hand the executor a fresh view."
   220	        )
   221	    elif v["H1_pass"]:
   222	        mechanism = (
   223	            "For R5, pinning the destination-reading heads kept the tapes the clock gets right, and putting Π "
   224	            "back every 256 tokens did not beat the plain forward."
   225	        )
   226	    elif v["H3_pass"]:
   227	        mechanism = (
   228	            "For R5, putting Π back every 256 tokens beat the plain forward at 8192, and pinning the committee did not."
   229	        )
   230	    else:
   231	        mechanism = (
   232	            "For R5, neither intervention is a substitute for the clock. Pinning the destination-reading heads "
   233	            "does not keep the tapes pin-all gets right, and a same-sized random mask is not better than leaving "
   234	            "every head free. Putting Π back every 256 tokens does not beat the plain forward."
   235	        )
   236	    h4m = v["H4"]
   237	    if h4m.get("falling") and h4m.get("restored"):
   238	        delay = "The distant medians fall with delay and relocation restores them at delay 4096, which is the margin lemma's pattern on these tables."
   239	    elif h4m.get("restored"):
   240	        delay = (
   241	            "Moving Π next to the query still raises S and both margins at delay 4096, so the table beside the "
   242	            "query matters, but the distant medians do not fall strictly with delay on these tables."
   243	        )
   244	    elif h4m.get("falling"):
   245	        delay = "The distant medians fall with delay, and relocation does not restore them at delay 4096."
   246	    else:
   247	        delay = "The distant medians do not fall strictly with delay, and relocation does not restore them at delay 4096."
   248	    return f"{opening} {mechanism} {delay}"
   249	
   250	
   251	def _replace(text, name, body):
   252	    start, end = f"<!-- {name}:START -->", f"<!-- {name}:END -->"
   253	    if start not in text or end not in text:
   254	        raise SystemExit(f"missing marker {name}")
   255	    pre, rest = text.split(start, 1)
   256	    _, post = rest.split(end, 1)
   257	    return pre + start + "\n" + body.rstrip() + "\n" + end + post
   258	
   259	
   260	def render(root):
   261	    root = pathlib.Path(root)
   262	    way_a, way_b = recount_runs(root)
   263	    if len(way_a) != len(way_b) or any(not _same_run(a, b) for a, b in zip(way_a, way_b)):
   264	        raise SystemExit("run recounts disagree")
   265	    e0_path = root / "e0.jsonl"
   266	    if not e0_path.exists():
   267	        raise SystemExit("no e0.jsonl")
   268	    ea, eb = recount_e0(e0_path)
   269	    if len(ea) != len(eb) or any(not _same_e0(a, b) for a, b in zip(ea, eb)):
   270	        raise SystemExit("e0 recounts disagree")
   271	    rows = way_b
   272	    v = verdicts(rows, eb)
   273	    run = json.loads((root / "run.json").read_text())
   274	    e0_meta = json.loads((root / "e0_meta.json").read_text())
   275	    ledger = json.loads((HERE / "results" / "modal_ledger.json").read_text())
   276	    jobs = []
   277	    for jid, j in ledger["jobs"].items():
   278	        jobs.append(f"- `{jid}`: status {j['status']}, gpu {j.get('gpu_actual', j['gpu'])}, "
   279	                    f"cpu {j.get('cpu_actual', 'not settled')}, "
   280	                    f"settled {_fmt(j.get('settled_usd', j['reserved_usd']))} dollars")
   281	    host = run.get("host", {})
   282	    smoke_path = root / "smoke.json"
   283	    smoke_txt = ""
   284	    if smoke_path.exists():
   285	        sm = json.loads(smoke_path.read_text())
   286	        ident = sm["identity"]
   287	        smoke_txt = (
   288	            f"Smoke identity pass: {_fmt(ident['pass'])}. "
   289	            f"All-heads logits equal: {_fmt(ident['all_heads_logits_equal'])} "
   290	            f"(max abs {_fmt(ident['all_heads_max_abs'])}). "
   291	            f"No-heads hidden equal: {_fmt(ident['no_heads_hidden_equal'])} "
   292	            f"(max abs {_fmt(ident['no_heads_max_abs'])}). "
   293	            f"Layer-0 block equal: {_fmt(ident['layer0_block_equal'])}. "
   294	            f"Mask equal: {_fmt(ident['mask_equal'])}. "
   295	            f"Standard-path equal: {_fmt(ident['std_equal'])}. "
   296	            f"One parity tape of length 1024 took {_fmt(sm['time_full_1024'])} s in full and "
   297	            f"{_fmt(sm['time_pin_committee_1024'])} s in pin-committee. "
   298	            f"Census max tokens {_fmt(sm['census_max_tokens'])}, sequences over the cap {_fmt(sm['census_over'])}."
   299	        )
   300	    n_expected = run["n"] * len(run["families"]) * len(run["lengths"]) * len(run["policies"])
   301	    summary = (
   302	        f"State-exact is the argmax of every teacher-forced state token. "
   303	        f"H1 {'passes' if v['H1_pass'] else 'fails'}. "
   304	        f"H2 {'passes' if v['H2_pass'] else 'fails'}. "
   305	        f"H3 {'passes' if v['H3_pass'] else 'fails'}. "
   306	        f"H4 {'passes' if v['H4_pass'] else 'fails'}. "
   307	        f"Counted rows {len(rows)} (the run file's grid is {n_expected}). "
   308	        f"E0 rows {len(eb)}. Seed in the run file {run['seed']}. Seed in the E0 file {e0_meta['seed']}."
   309	    )
   310	    results = "\n\n".join([
   311	        f"Seed {run['seed']}. Families {', '.join(run['families'])}. Tapes per family and length: {run['n']}.",
   312	        f"Host recorded in the run file: `{json.dumps(host)}`.",
   313	        "Ledger:",
   314	        "\n".join(jobs) if jobs else "- no jobs",
   315	        smoke_txt,
   316	        "Policies by length. State-exact and read-exact are counts of tapes. "
   317	        "pin-all has no read tokens, so its read-exact cell is empty. "
   318	        "First read and first step count tapes whose earliest wrong run token is that kind. "
   319	        "Earliest step is the smallest such step in the cell, empty when every tape is exact. "
   320	        "The median min margin is the median across tapes of each tape's minimum state-token margin.",
   321	        _table(rows),
   322	        "E0. Each median is over the 4 tables. For an even count, the median is the mean of the two central values. "
   323	        "Distant is Π then the trace then the query. Relocated is the trace then Π then the query.",
   324	        _e0_table(v["H4"]),
   325	        f"H4 falling: {_fmt(v['H4'].get('falling'))}. H4 restored at delay 4096: {_fmt(v['H4'].get('restored'))}.",
   326	    ])
   327	    return summary, results, _r5(v), smoke_txt, v
   328	
   329	
   330	def main():
   331	    root = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else HERE / "results" / "counted"
   332	    doc = HERE / "HEADPIN.md"
   333	    summary, results, r5, smoke_txt, _v = render(root)
   334	    text = doc.read_text()
   335	    text = _replace(text, "SUMMARY", summary)
   336	    text = _replace(text, "RESULTS", results)
   337	    text = _replace(text, "R5", r5)
   338	    # leave a hand-written smoke note alone
   339	    start = "<!-- SMOKE:START -->"
   340	    inner = text.split(start, 1)[1].split("<!-- SMOKE:END -->", 1)[0]
   341	    if inner.strip() == "Pending smoke." and (root / "smoke.json").exists():
   342	        sm = json.loads((root / "smoke.json").read_text())
   343	        if sm["identity"]["pass"]:
   344	            text = _replace(text, "SMOKE", "The smoke identity checks passed. No prediction is superseded.")
   345	    doc.write_text(text)
   346	    print(json.dumps({"wrote": str(doc), "rows_ok": True}))
   347	
   348	
   349	if __name__ == "__main__":
   350	    main()

     1	"""Per-head attention for the head-level pin.
     2	
     3	Committee heads attend with the clock's key set and the clock's positions. Every other
     4	head keeps the ordinary causal forward. Two shapes are fixed so the extremes can match
     5	bitwise:
     6	
     7	- No heads: the original forward.
     8	- Every head: each layer runs on Π as (1, |Π|, d), then on each block as (1, 2, d),
     9	  the prefill's shape and the tick's shape. The residual MLP is a GEMM, so a
    10	  full-sequence product is not bitwise with those two shapes.
    11	- A subset of heads is patched inside one full-sequence forward. That kernel is not
    12	  the one the identity check compares to the clock.
    13	"""
    14	
    15	from __future__ import annotations
    16	
    17	import hp_heads
    18	
    19	
    20	class _Groups1:
    21	    num_key_value_groups = 1
    22	    is_causal = True
    23	    layer_idx = 0
    24	    training = False
    25	
    26	
    27	def _imports():
    28	    import torch
    29	    from transformers.integrations.sdpa_attention import sdpa_attention_forward
    30	    from transformers.models.qwen3.modeling_qwen3 import apply_rotary_pos_emb
    31	    return torch, sdpa_attention_forward, apply_rotary_pos_emb
