    33	
    34	def _past_len(past):
    35	    """How many tokens a cache already holds. An empty cache is not the clock's tick."""
    36	    if past is None:
    37	        return 0
    38	    get = getattr(past, "get_seq_length", None)
    39	    if get is None:
    40	        return 1
    41	    try:
    42	        return int(get())
    43	    except Exception:
    44	        return 1
    45	
    46	
    47	def repeat_kv(x, n_rep):
    48	    if n_rep == 1:
    49	        return x
    50	    b, kv, s, d = x.shape
    51	    return x[:, :, None].expand(b, kv, n_rep, s, d).reshape(b, kv * n_rep, s, d)
    52	
    53	
    54	def project(module, hidden):
    55	    """q, k, v from layernormed hidden (B, S, d), the same three lines as Qwen3Attention."""
    56	    b, s, _ = hidden.shape
    57	    hd = module.head_dim
    58	    q = module.q_norm(module.q_proj(hidden).view(b, s, -1, hd)).transpose(1, 2)
    59	    k = module.k_norm(module.k_proj(hidden).view(b, s, -1, hd)).transpose(1, 2)
    60	    v = module.v_proj(hidden).view(b, s, -1, hd).transpose(1, 2)
    61	    return q, k, v
    62	
    63	
    64	def project_q(module, hidden):
    65	    b, s, _ = hidden.shape
    66	    hd = module.head_dim
    67	    return module.q_norm(module.q_proj(hidden).view(b, s, -1, hd)).transpose(1, 2)
    68	
    69	
    70	def rope(rotary, apply_rope, q, k, position_ids):
    71	    cos, sin = rotary(q, position_ids)
    72	    return apply_rope(q, k, cos, sin)
    73	
    74	
    75	def clock_mask(torch, P, device):
    76	    kv = torch.arange(P + 2, device=device)
    77	    q = torch.tensor([P, P + 1], device=device)
    78	    return (kv[None, :] <= q[:, None]).view(1, 1, 2, P + 2)
    79	
    80	
    81	def standard_attn(module, hidden, position_embeddings, attention_mask, sdpa, apply_rope, kwargs):
    82	    q, k, v = project(module, hidden)
    83	    cos, sin = position_embeddings
    84	    q, k = apply_rope(q, k, cos, sin)
    85	    # the decoder forwards position_ids and use_cache; sdpa ignores them. Drop names that are
    86	    # already passed explicitly so a future kwarg cannot raise.
    87	    skip = {"dropout", "scaling", "sliding_window", "is_causal", "position_bias", "cache", "output_attentions"}
    88	    kw = {k: v for k, v in kwargs.items() if k not in skip}
    89	    out, _ = sdpa(
    90	        module, q, k, v, attention_mask,
    91	        dropout=0.0 if not module.training else module.attention_dropout,
    92	        scaling=module.scaling,
    93	        sliding_window=getattr(module, "sliding_window", None),
    94	        **kw,
    95	    )
    96	    return out  # (B, S, H, D)
    97	
    98	
    99	def _scatter_heads(out, positions, head_idx, values, all_pinned):
   100	    """values (1, S, H, D) or (S, Hs, D) written onto out (1, N, H, D)."""
   101	    if all_pinned:
   102	        out[0, positions] = values[0]
   103	        return
   104	    # values (S, Hs, D)
   105	    s = positions.shape[0]
   106	    hs = head_idx.shape[0]
   107	    idx_n = positions[:, None].expand(s, hs)
   108	    idx_h = head_idx[None, :].expand(s, hs)
   109	    out[0, idx_n, idx_h] = values
   110	
   111	
   112	def _sdpa_heads(sdpa, module, q, k, v, head_idx, all_pinned, attn_mask, is_causal):
   113	    """q (B, H, S, D), k/v (B, KV, S, D) or already head-expanded when not all_pinned."""
   114	    if all_pinned:
   115	        out, _ = sdpa(module, q, k, v, attn_mask, dropout=0.0, scaling=module.scaling, is_causal=is_causal)
   116	        return out
   117	    groups = module.num_key_value_groups
   118	    k_h = repeat_kv(k, groups)
   119	    v_h = repeat_kv(v, groups)
   120	    out, _ = sdpa(
   121	        _Groups1(), q[:, head_idx], k_h[:, head_idx], v_h[:, head_idx], attn_mask,
   122	        dropout=0.0, scaling=module.scaling, is_causal=is_causal,
   123	    )
   124	    return out
   125	
   126	
   127	def fill_pinned(out, hidden, module, spec, heads, all_pinned, sdpa, apply_rope):
   128	    import torch as T
   129	    rotary = spec.rotary
   130	    device = hidden.device
   131	    pi = spec.pi_pos
   132	    P = int(pi.shape[0])
   133	    head_idx = T.tensor(heads, device=device)
   134	    h_pi = hidden.index_select(1, pi)
   135	    q, k, v = project(module, h_pi)
   136	    pos = T.arange(P, device=device)[None]
   137	    q, k = rope(rotary, apply_rope, q, k, pos)
   138	    pi_out = _sdpa_heads(sdpa, module, q, k, v, head_idx, all_pinned, None, None)
   139	    if all_pinned:
   140	        _scatter_heads(out, pi, head_idx, pi_out, True)
   141	    else:
   142	        _scatter_heads(out, pi, head_idx, pi_out[0], False)
   143	    # k, v stay at KV-head width for the block cat, matching the cache.
   144	    k_pi, v_pi = k, v
   145	
   146	    state, event = spec.state_pos, spec.event_pos
   147	    n_steps = int(state.shape[0])
   148	    mask = clock_mask(T, P, device)
   149	    if all_pinned:
   150	        block = hidden.new_empty(n_steps, 2, spec.n_heads, module.head_dim)
   151	        for t in range(n_steps):
   152	            hb = hidden[:, [int(state[t]), int(event[t])], :]
   153	            qb, kb, vb = project(module, hb)
   154	            pb = T.tensor([[P, P + 1]], device=device)
   155	            qb, kb = rope(rotary, apply_rope, qb, kb, pb)
   156	            ob, _ = sdpa(
   157	                module, qb, T.cat([k_pi, kb], dim=2), T.cat([v_pi, vb], dim=2), mask,
   158	                dropout=0.0, scaling=module.scaling,
   159	            )
   160	            block[t] = ob[0]
   161	            if spec.debug is not None and t == 0:
   162	                spec.debug.setdefault("block_h", {})[module.layer_idx] = hb.detach().clone()
   163	                if module.layer_idx == 0:
   164	                    spec.debug["long_h0"] = spec.debug["block_h"][0]
   165	                    spec.debug["long_mask"] = mask.detach().clone()
   166	                    spec.debug["long_block0"] = ob.detach().clone()
   167	        out[0, state] = block[:, 0]
   168	        out[0, event] = block[:, 1]
   169	        # Positions outside Π and the block are not read by either, at any layer, when every
   170	        # head is pinned. Leaving them zero keeps the checked logits on the clock's path.
   171	        return
   172	    _fill_blocks_batched(out, hidden, module, spec, head_idx, k_pi, v_pi, P, mask, sdpa, apply_rope, T)
   173	    _fill_other(out, hidden, module, spec, head_idx, k_pi, v_pi, P, sdpa, apply_rope, T)
   174	
   175	
   176	def _fill_blocks_batched(out, hidden, module, spec, head_idx, k_pi, v_pi, P, mask, sdpa, apply_rope, T):
   177	    state, event = spec.state_pos, spec.event_pos
   178	    n = int(state.shape[0])
   179	    # (n, 2, d) in clock order [state, event]
   180	    hb = T.stack([hidden[0].index_select(0, state), hidden[0].index_select(0, event)], dim=1)
   181	    groups = module.num_key_value_groups
   182	    k_pi_h = repeat_kv(k_pi, groups)[:, head_idx]  # (1, Hs, P, D)
   183	    v_pi_h = repeat_kv(v_pi, groups)[:, head_idx]
   184	    hs = int(head_idx.shape[0])
   185	    chunk = 128
   186	    for c in range(0, n, chunk):
   187	        sl = slice(c, min(n, c + chunk))
   188	        m = hb[sl]
   189	        q, k, v = project(module, m)
   190	        pos = T.tensor([[P, P + 1]], device=hidden.device).expand(m.shape[0], 2)
   191	        q, k = rope(spec.rotary, apply_rope, q, k, pos)
   192	        q = q[:, head_idx]
   193	        k = repeat_kv(k, groups)[:, head_idx]
   194	        v = repeat_kv(v, groups)[:, head_idx]
   195	        kk = T.cat([k_pi_h.expand(m.shape[0], -1, -1, -1), k], dim=2)
   196	        vv = T.cat([v_pi_h.expand(m.shape[0], -1, -1, -1), v], dim=2)
   197	        ob, _ = sdpa(_Groups1(), q, kk, vv, mask, dropout=0.0, scaling=module.scaling)
   198	        # ob (chunk, 2, Hs, D)
   199	        st, ev = state[sl], event[sl]
   200	        idx_h = head_idx[None, :].expand(m.shape[0], hs)
   201	        out[0, st[:, None].expand_as(idx_h), idx_h] = ob[:, 0]
   202	        out[0, ev[:, None].expand_as(idx_h), idx_h] = ob[:, 1]
   203	
   204	
   205	def _fill_other(out, hidden, module, spec, head_idx, k_pi, v_pi, P, sdpa, apply_rope, T):
   206	    """Pinned heads outside Π and the block read the whole table and nothing else.
   207	    The query is rotated at clock position |Π|, one step after the table."""
   208	    n = hidden.shape[1]
   209	    special = T.zeros(n, dtype=T.bool, device=hidden.device)
   210	    special[spec.pi_pos] = True
   211	    special[spec.state_pos] = True
   212	    special[spec.event_pos] = True
   213	    other = (~special).nonzero(as_tuple=False).flatten()
   214	    if other.numel() == 0:
   215	        return
   216	    groups = module.num_key_value_groups
   217	    k_h = repeat_kv(k_pi, groups)[:, head_idx]
   218	    v_h = repeat_kv(v_pi, groups)[:, head_idx]
   219	    hs = int(head_idx.shape[0])
   220	    # chunk so a long prompt does not materialise every query head at once
   221	    for c in range(0, int(other.numel()), 512):
   222	        sl = other[c:c + 512]
   223	        q = project_q(module, hidden.index_select(1, sl))
   224	        pos = T.full((1, int(sl.numel())), P, device=hidden.device, dtype=T.long)
   225	        cos, sin = spec.rotary(q, pos)
   226	        q, _ = apply_rope(q, q, cos, sin)
   227	        ob, _ = sdpa(
   228	            _Groups1(), q[:, head_idx], k_h, v_h, None,
   229	            dropout=0.0, scaling=module.scaling, is_causal=False,
   230	        )
   231	        idx_h = head_idx[None, :].expand(int(sl.numel()), hs)
   232	        out[0, sl[:, None].expand_as(idx_h), idx_h] = ob[0]
   233	
   234	
   235	class Pin:
   236	    """Installed on the model. `spec` None, or a Spec, selects the policy for the next forward."""
   237	
   238	    def __init__(self, model):
   239	        torch, sdpa, apply_rope = _imports()
   240	        self.torch = torch
   241	        self.sdpa = sdpa
   242	        self.apply_rope = apply_rope
   243	        self.spec = None
   244	        self.model = model
   245	        self._orig = []
   246	        rotary = model.model.rotary_emb
   247	        for layer in model.model.layers:
   248	            attn = layer.self_attn
   249	            orig = attn.forward
   250	            self._orig.append((attn, orig))
   251	            attn.forward = self._make(attn, orig, rotary)
   252	
   253	    def _make(self, module, orig, rotary):
   254	        pin = self
   255	
   256	        def forward(hidden_states, position_embeddings, attention_mask, past_key_values=None, **kwargs):
   257	            spec = pin.spec
   258	            # A cache that already holds Π is the clock's tick. An empty cache (or none) is a plain
   259	            # forward, which is the one the pin replaces. use_cache=False passes None.
   260	            if spec is None or _past_len(past_key_values) > 0:
   261	                return orig(
   262	                    hidden_states=hidden_states, position_embeddings=position_embeddings,
   263	                    attention_mask=attention_mask, past_key_values=past_key_values, **kwargs,
   264	                )
   265	            heads = spec.heads_by_layer.get(module.layer_idx, [])
   266	            if spec.std_only or not heads:
   267	                if spec.std_only and module.layer_idx == 0 and spec.debug is not None:
   268	                    custom = standard_attn(
   269	                        module, hidden_states, position_embeddings, attention_mask,
   270	                        pin.sdpa, pin.apply_rope, kwargs,
   271	                    )
   272	                    got = orig(
   273	                        hidden_states=hidden_states, position_embeddings=position_embeddings,
   274	                        attention_mask=attention_mask, past_key_values=None, **kwargs,
   275	                    )[0]
   276	                    # compare pre-o_proj by undoing nothing: compare the returned hidden after o_proj
   277	                    # against a custom o_proj, one layer only, as a diagnostic
   278	                    custom_y = module.o_proj(custom.reshape(hidden_states.shape[0], hidden_states.shape[1], -1).contiguous())
   279	                    spec.debug["std_equal"] = bool(pin.torch.equal(custom_y, got))
   280	                    if not spec.debug["std_equal"]:
   281	                        spec.debug["std_max"] = float((custom_y.float() - got.float()).abs().max())
   282	                return orig(
   283	                    hidden_states=hidden_states, position_embeddings=position_embeddings,
   284	                    attention_mask=attention_mask, past_key_values=past_key_values, **kwargs,
   285	                )
   286	            all_pinned = len(heads) == spec.n_heads and list(heads) == list(range(spec.n_heads))
   287	            b, n, _ = hidden_states.shape
   288	            if all_pinned:
   289	                attn = hidden_states.new_zeros(b, n, spec.n_heads, module.head_dim)
   290	            else:
   291	                attn = standard_attn(
   292	                    module, hidden_states, position_embeddings, attention_mask,
   293	                    pin.sdpa, pin.apply_rope, kwargs,
   294	                )
   295	            spec.rotary = rotary
   296	            fill_pinned(attn, hidden_states, module, spec, heads, all_pinned, pin.sdpa, pin.apply_rope)
   297	            y = module.o_proj(attn.reshape(b, n, -1).contiguous())
   298	            return y, None
   299	
   300	        return forward
   301	
   302	    def close(self):
   303	        for attn, orig in self._orig:
   304	            attn.forward = orig
   305	        self._orig.clear()
   306	
   307	
   308	class Spec:
   309	    def __init__(self, pairs, pi_pos, state_pos, event_pos, n_heads, debug=False, std_only=False):
   310	        import torch
   311	        self.heads_by_layer = hp_heads.by_layer(pairs)
   312	        self.pi_pos = torch.as_tensor(pi_pos, dtype=torch.long)
   313	        self.state_pos = torch.as_tensor(state_pos, dtype=torch.long)
   314	        self.event_pos = torch.as_tensor(event_pos, dtype=torch.long)
   315	        self.n_heads = n_heads
   316	        self.debug = {} if debug else None
   317	        self.std_only = std_only
   318	        self.rotary = None
   319	
   320	    def to(self, device):
   321	        self.pi_pos = self.pi_pos.to(device)
   322	        self.state_pos = self.state_pos.to(device)
   323	        self.event_pos = self.event_pos.to(device)
   324	        return self
   325	
   326	
   327	def pairs_all(n_layers, n_heads):
   328	    return hp_heads.all_heads(n_layers, n_heads)
   329	
   330	
   331	def is_all_pinned(spec):
   332	    if spec is None or spec.std_only:
   333	        return False
   334	    full = list(range(spec.n_heads))
   335	    layers = spec.heads_by_layer
   336	    return len(layers) == hp_heads.N_LAYERS and all(
   337	        list(layers.get(i, [])) == full for i in range(hp_heads.N_LAYERS))
   338	
   339	
   340	def _hidden_of(out):
   341	    return out if hasattr(out, "shape") else out[0]
   342	
   343	
   344	def forward_all_pinned(model, ids, spec):
   345	    """Π at clock positions 0..|Π|-1, then each [state, event] block at |Π|, |Π|+1.
   346	
   347	    The gathered token vectors are the same embeddings the clock looks up. Each layer
   348	    is the real decoder layer, so every linear has the clock's batch shape.
   349	    """
   350	    import torch
   351	    import pb_pin
   352	    from transformers.cache_utils import DynamicCache
   353	
   354	    core = model.model
   355	    device = next(model.parameters()).device
   356	    hidden = core.embed_tokens(torch.tensor([list(ids)], device=device))
   357	    pi = spec.pi_pos.to(device)
   358	    state = spec.state_pos.to(device)
   359	    event = spec.event_pos.to(device)
   360	    P, T = int(pi.numel()), int(state.numel())
   361	    pos_pi = torch.arange(P, device=device)[None]
   362	    pos_b = torch.tensor([[P, P + 1]], device=device)
   363	    mask = clock_mask(torch, P, device)
   364	    hooks = []
   365	    if spec.debug is not None:
   366	        spec.debug["block_h"] = {}
   367	        spec.debug["path"] = "sliced_layer"
   368	        for layer in core.layers:
   369	            attn, orig, box = layer.self_attn, layer.self_attn.forward, {"n": 0}
   370	
   371	            def wrapped(hidden_states, position_embeddings, attention_mask, past_key_values=None,
   372	                        _attn=attn, _orig=orig, _box=box, **kw):
   373	                _box["n"] += 1
   374	                if _box["n"] == 2:
   375	                    spec.debug["block_h"][_attn.layer_idx] = hidden_states.detach().clone()
   376	                    if _attn.layer_idx == 0:
   377	                        spec.debug["long_h0"] = spec.debug["block_h"][0]
   378	                        spec.debug["long_mask"] = None if attention_mask is None else attention_mask.detach().clone()
   379	                out = _orig(hidden_states=hidden_states, position_embeddings=position_embeddings,
   380	                            attention_mask=attention_mask, past_key_values=past_key_values, **kw)
   381	                if _box["n"] == 2 and _attn.layer_idx == 0:
   382	                    spec.debug["layer0_y"] = out[0].detach().clone()
   383	                return out
   384	
   385	            attn.forward = wrapped
   386	            hooks.append((attn, orig))
   387	    try:
   388	        for layer in core.layers:
   389	            try:
   390	                cache = DynamicCache(config=model.config)
   391	            except TypeError:
   392	                cache = DynamicCache()
   393	            h_pi = hidden.index_select(1, pi).contiguous()
   394	            h_pi = _hidden_of(layer(
   395	                h_pi, attention_mask=None, position_ids=pos_pi, past_key_values=cache,
   396	                use_cache=True, position_embeddings=core.rotary_emb(h_pi, pos_pi),
   397	                cache_position=pos_pi[0],
   398	            ))
   399	            hidden[:, pi] = h_pi
   400	            for t in range(T):
   401	                idx = torch.stack((state[t], event[t]))
   402	                h_b = hidden.index_select(1, idx).contiguous()
   403	                h_b = _hidden_of(layer(
   404	                    h_b, attention_mask=mask, position_ids=pos_b,
   405	                    past_key_values=pb_pin._clone(cache), use_cache=True,
   406	                    position_embeddings=core.rotary_emb(h_b, pos_b), cache_position=pos_b[0],
   407	                ))
   408	                hidden[0, state[t]] = h_b[0, 0]
   409	                hidden[0, event[t]] = h_b[0, 1]
   410	    finally:
   411	        for attn, orig in hooks:
   412	            attn.forward = orig
   413	    return core.norm(hidden)[0]
     1	"""Measured lookup committee and the random control.
     2	
     3	The committee is CIRCUITS' destination-reading set: survey-final heads with matched-row
     4	mass above 1/2 whose strongest slot on the matched row is the destination token t
     5	(`ablate_spec.json` extra.t_heads, 55 held-out tables, seed 3030 for the ablation
     6	counts; the head map is the survey, seed 2026). CIRCUITS calls this the committee of
     7	about 30 destination-reading heads. L7.H7 is the base model's finder; it reads the
     8	event token and is not in this set.
     9	
    10	The random control is 31 heads drawn from the other 1,121, seed 6060, then sorted.
    11	"""
    12	
    13	from __future__ import annotations
    14	
    15	N_LAYERS, N_HEADS = 36, 32
    16	
    17	# (layer, head), survey-final t-readers, in the survey's rank order.
    18	COMMITTEE = (
    19	    (24, 23), (22, 23), (22, 22), (20, 21), (24, 22), (23, 4), (29, 11), (24, 5),
    20	    (13, 13), (23, 5), (22, 21), (24, 20), (29, 12), (16, 21), (29, 9), (23, 10),
    21	    (22, 4), (23, 30), (21, 18), (24, 13), (13, 15), (21, 22), (23, 12), (24, 8),
    22	    (28, 0), (20, 30), (24, 15), (23, 21), (20, 14), (29, 14), (33, 29),
    23	)
    24	
    25	RANDOM_SEED = 6060
    26	
    27	# sample(seed 6060) from the complement, then sorted so the set is what is stored.
    28	RANDOM = (
    29	    (0, 5), (0, 6), (2, 10), (4, 6), (5, 25), (6, 19), (7, 2), (9, 2), (10, 12),
    30	    (10, 13), (10, 21), (11, 7), (11, 18), (12, 21), (14, 13), (14, 31), (16, 7),
    31	    (17, 30), (18, 8), (18, 20), (19, 11), (20, 19), (21, 1), (27, 27), (32, 1),
    32	    (32, 23), (33, 4), (33, 14), (34, 5), (34, 16), (35, 2),
    33	)

    36	def by_layer(pairs):
    37	    out = {}
    38	    for layer, head in pairs:
    39	        out.setdefault(layer, []).append(head)
    40	    for layer in out:
    41	        out[layer] = sorted(set(out[layer]))
    42	    return out
    43	
    44	
    45	def all_heads(n_layers=N_LAYERS, n_heads=N_HEADS):
    46	    return tuple((layer, head) for layer in range(n_layers) for head in range(n_heads))
    47	
    48	
    49	def check():
    50	    committee, random = set(COMMITTEE), set(RANDOM)
    51	    assert len(COMMITTEE) == 31 and len(committee) == 31
    52	    assert len(RANDOM) == 31 and len(random) == 31
    53	    assert committee.isdisjoint(random)
    54	    assert committee | random <= set(all_heads())
    55	    # regenerating the draw must reproduce RANDOM
    56	    import random as pyrandom
    57	    pool = [h for h in all_heads() if h not in committee]
    58	    draw = pyrandom.Random(RANDOM_SEED).sample(pool, 31)
    59	    assert set(draw) == random
     1	"""Sequence edits that do not need a model: requote splicing, the clock mask, E0 pairs, S(n)."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	
     6	def splice_before(ids, inserts, at):
     7	    """Insert `inserts` immediately before each original index in `at` (a set).
     8	    Returns (new_ids, old_to_new) where old_to_new[i] is the new index of original token i."""
     9	    at = set(at)
    10	    block = list(inserts)
    11	    out, mp = [], {}
    12	    for i, tok in enumerate(ids):
    13	        if i in at:
    14	            out.extend(block)
    15	        mp[i] = len(out)
    16	        out.append(tok)
    17	    return out, mp
    18	
    19	
    20	def requote_positions(n, pi_end, run_start, run_len, W):
    21	    """Original indices before which a fresh Π is inserted.
    22	
    23	    Counting starts at the end of the first Π (`pi_end`) and walks every later token,
    24	    including the rest of the program and the run. A copy is inserted every W of those
    25	    tokens. Insertions that would fall past the run are not made: the lag that matters
    26	    is along the execution, and tokens after the run are not scored.
    27	    """
    28	    if W <= 0:
    29	        raise ValueError(W)
    30	    at, seen = [], 0
    31	    stop = run_start + run_len
    32	    for i in range(pi_end, n):
    33	        if seen == W and i <= stop:
    34	            at.append(i)
    35	            seen = 0
    36	        if i == stop:
    37	            break
    38	        seen += 1
    39	    return at
    40	
    41	
    42	def clock_mask_rows(P):
    43	    """Bool rows (attend = True) for the two block queries over Π + block. Query 0 is the
    44	    state at clock position P; query 1 is the event at P+1. Keys are 0 .. P+1."""
    45	    rows = []
    46	    for q in (P, P + 1):
    47	        rows.append([kv <= q for kv in range(P + 2)])
    48	    return rows
    49	
    50	
    51	def multiset(ids):
    52	    from collections import Counter
    53	    return Counter(ids)
    54	
    55	
    56	def s_value(z_uu, z_uv, z_vu, z_vv):
    57	    """S(n) = (z_u[u] - z_u[v]) - (z_v[u] - z_v[v])."""
    58	    return (z_uu - z_uv) - (z_vu - z_vv)
    59	
    60	
    61	def margin_from_logits(logits, target):
    62	    """Target logit minus the best other logit. `logits` is a sequence of numbers."""
    63	    t = logits[target]
    64	    best = None
    65	    for i, v in enumerate(logits):
    66	        if i == target:
    67	            continue
    68	        if best is None or v > best:
    69	            best = v
    70	    return t - best

     1	"""Frozen tables, tapes, and one policy's teacher-forced scores.
     2	
     3	Three single-register families (parity, cups, compass). One program is frozen per family — the first
     4	training draw, seed 4040, for which the clock can read every tape — and the eight tapes at each length
     5	only resample that program's events. Robot is not used: it has two registers, and the block in the brief
     6	is one (state, event).
     7	
     8	Exactness for the predictions is the state tokens (events supplied). Read tokens are still scored, and
     9	the first error is whichever of the two is earlier.
    10	"""
    11	
    12	from __future__ import annotations
    13	
    14	import pathlib
    15	import random
    16	import sys
    17	
    18	HERE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    19	for _p in (HERE, HERE.parent / "bridge", HERE.parent / "promptbox", "/root/hp", "/root/br", "/root/pb"):
    20	    if pathlib.Path(_p).exists() and str(_p) not in sys.path:
    21	        sys.path.insert(0, str(_p))

    31	FAMILIES = ("parity", "cups", "compass")

    43	def limit():
    44	    return C.CONTEXT - MARGIN

    47	def _dev():
    48	    import torch
    49	    return "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"

    51	
    52	def geometry(tok, lay):
    53	    """Positions of Π, of the state that enters each step, and of the event of each step."""
    54	    if lay["R"] != 1 or lay["stride"] != 2:
    55	        raise RuntimeError(f"expected one register, got R={lay['R']}")
    56	    rows = lay["rows"][0]
    57	    pi_pos = []
    58	    for row in rows:
    59	        pi_pos += [row["pos"] + j for j in range(4)]
    60	    if pi_pos != list(range(pi_pos[0], pi_pos[0] + len(pi_pos))):
    61	        raise RuntimeError("Π is not one contiguous span")
    62	    nl = C.enc(tok, "\n")
    63	    start_pos = rows[0]["pos"] - 1 - len(nl)
    64	    state_pos, event_pos = [], []
    65	    r0, st = lay["run_start"], lay["stride"]
    66	    for k in range(lay["T"]):
    67	        event_pos.append(r0 + k * st)
    68	        state_pos.append(start_pos if k == 0 else r0 + (k - 1) * st + 1)
    69	    return {"pi_pos": pi_pos, "pi_end": pi_pos[-1] + 1, "start_pos": start_pos,
    70	            "state_pos": state_pos, "event_pos": event_pos}
    71	
    72	
    73	def _check_layout(tok, lay, geo):
    74	    ids = lay["ids"]
    75	    nl = C.enc(tok, "\n")
    76	    if ids[geo["start_pos"]:geo["start_pos"] + 1 + len(nl)] != [lay["starts"][0]] + nl:
    77	        raise RuntimeError("start token is not where the geometry says")
    78	    pi = []
    79	    for row in lay["rows"][0]:
    80	        chunk = [row["s"], row["e"], row["t"]] + nl
    81	        if ids[row["pos"]:row["pos"] + 4] != chunk:
    82	            raise RuntimeError("row tokens moved")
    83	        pi += chunk
    84	    if [ids[i] for i in geo["pi_pos"]] != pi:
    85	        raise RuntimeError("Π positions do not match the row tokens")
    86	    r0, st = lay["run_start"], lay["stride"]
    87	    prev = lay["starts"][0]
    88	    for k in range(lay["T"]):
    89	        ev = ids[r0 + k * st]
    90	        wrote = ids[r0 + k * st + 1]
    91	        if ids[geo["event_pos"][k]] != ev or ids[geo["state_pos"][k]] != prev:
    92	            raise RuntimeError(f"step {k} positions disagree with the trace")
    93	        if ids[geo["event_pos"][k] + 1] != wrote:
    94	            raise RuntimeError(f"step {k} state is not the token after the event")
    95	        prev = wrote
    96	    return pi
    97	
    98	
    99	def _example(frozen, items):
   100	    a, b = frozen["span"]
   101	    q = frozen["question"][:a] + render_stream(items, frozen["fmt"]) + frozen["question"][b:]
   102	    return {"kind": "family", "prompt": chat_prompt(q), "question": q, "prog_text": frozen["prog_text"],
   103	            "program_in_prompt": False, "items": items, "L": len(items), "family": frozen["family"],
   104	            "phrasing": frozen["phrasing"], "fmt": frozen["fmt"], "answer": frozen["answer"]}
   105	
   106	
   107	def _tape_from(tok, frozen, items, L, i):
   108	    ex = _example(frozen, items)
   109	    lit, why = pb_lit.literal(ex, tok)
   110	    if lit is None:
   111	        return None, why
   112	    if len(lit["registers"]) != 1:
   113	        return None, "more than one register"
   114	    lay = C.layout(tok, ex, lit)
   115	    geo = geometry(tok, lay)
   116	    pi = _check_layout(tok, lay, geo)
   117	    return {"family": frozen["family"], "L": L, "i": i, "seed": SEED, "lay": lay, "geo": geo, "pi": pi}, None
   118	
   119	
   120	def _draw_tape(tok, frozen, L, i, redraws=5):
   121	    events = frozen["events"]
   122	    last = None
   123	    for t in range(redraws):
   124	        # t = 0 is the registered tape. A later try is only used when the clock cannot read that draw.
   125	        label = f"{SEED}|{frozen['family']}|{L}|{i}" if t == 0 else f"{SEED}|{frozen['family']}|{L}|{i}|redraw|{t}"
   126	        rng = random.Random(label)
   127	        items = [rng.choice(events) for _ in range(L)]
   128	        tape, why = _tape_from(tok, frozen, items, L, i)
   129	        if tape is not None:
   130	            tape["draw"] = t
   131	            return tape, None
   132	        last = why
   133	    return None, last
   134	
   135	
   136	def requote_of(tape, W):
   137	    lay, geo = tape["lay"], tape["geo"]
   138	    at = hp_seq.requote_positions(lay["n"], geo["pi_end"], lay["run_start"], lay["T"] * lay["stride"], W)
   139	    ids, mp = hp_seq.splice_before(lay["ids"], tape["pi"], at)
   140	    return ids, mp, at
   141	
   142	
   143	def _fits(tape):
   144	    n = tape["lay"]["n"]
   145	    out = {"full": n}
   146	    cap = limit()
   147	    over = n > cap
   148	    for W in WS:
   149	        ids, _, _ = requote_of(tape, W)
   150	        out[f"requote-{W}"] = len(ids)
   151	        over = over or len(ids) > cap
   152	    out["over"] = over
   153	    return out
   154	
   155	
   156	def _meta_of(family, attempt, inst, lit, span):
   157	    a, b, _ = span
   158	    return {"family": family, "attempt": attempt, "fmt": inst.fmt, "phrasing": inst.phrasing,
   159	            "events": list(lit["events"]), "rows": len(lit["registers"][0]["rows"]),
   160	            "prog_text": inst.program_text, "question": inst.question, "span": (a, b),
   161	            "answer": inst.answer}
   162	
   163	
   164	def _frozen(meta):
   165	    return {"family": meta["family"], "attempt": meta["attempt"], "prog_text": meta["prog_text"],
   166	            "question": meta["question"], "fmt": meta["fmt"], "phrasing": meta["phrasing"],
   167	            "span": tuple(meta["span"]), "answer": meta["answer"], "events": list(meta["events"])}
   168	
   169	
   170	def freeze_family(tok, family, lengths=LENGTHS, n=N_TAPES):
   171	    """First single-register table whose every tape the clock can read and whose requote fits."""
   172	    for attempt in range(40):
   173	        rng = random.Random(f"{SEED}|{family}|table|{attempt}")
   174	        inst = make(family, rng, L=4, split="train")
   175	        span = pb_lit.item_span(inst.question, inst.items)
   176	        if span is None:
   177	            continue
   178	        ex = {"kind": "family", "prompt": chat_prompt(inst.question), "question": inst.question,
   179	              "prog_text": inst.program_text, "program_in_prompt": False, "items": inst.items,
   180	              "L": inst.length, "family": family, "phrasing": inst.phrasing, "fmt": inst.fmt,
   181	              "answer": inst.answer}
   182	        lit, _why = pb_lit.literal(ex, tok)
   183	        if lit is None or len(lit["registers"]) != 1:
   184	            continue
   185	        meta = _meta_of(family, attempt, inst, lit, span)
   186	        frozen = _frozen(meta)
   187	        tapes, ok = [], True
   188	        for L in sorted(lengths, reverse=True):
   189	            for i in range(n):
   190	                tape, _why = _draw_tape(tok, frozen, L, i)
   191	                if tape is None or _fits(tape)["over"]:
   192	                    ok = False
   193	                    break
   194	                tapes.append(tape)
   195	            if not ok:
   196	                break
   197	        if ok:
   198	            tapes.sort(key=lambda t: (t["L"], t["i"]))
   199	            return meta, tapes
   200	    raise RuntimeError(f"no {family} table fits in {limit()} tokens")
   201	
   202	
   203	def build_corpus(tok, families=FAMILIES, lengths=LENGTHS, n=N_TAPES):
   204	    metas, tapes = [], []
   205	    for fam in families:
   206	        meta, got = freeze_family(tok, fam, lengths, n)
   207	        metas.append(meta)
   208	        tapes.extend(got)
   209	    return metas, tapes
   210	
   211	
   212	def smoke_tape(tok, meta, L=8):
   213	    """A short tape of an already frozen table. Its generator is not a counted tape's generator."""
   214	    rng = random.Random(f"{SEED}|{meta['family']}|smoke|{L}")
   215	    items = [rng.choice(list(meta["events"])) for _ in range(L)]
   216	    tape, why = _tape_from(tok, _frozen(meta), items, L, -1)
   217	    if tape is None:
   218	        raise RuntimeError(f"smoke tape: {why}")
   219	    tape["role"] = "smoke"
   220	    return tape
   221

   223	def forward(model, ids, pin, spec):
   224	    import torch
   225	    import hp_attn
   226	    # every head pinned has to use the clock's GEMM shapes, including the residual MLP,
   227	    # which the attention wrapper cannot see
   228	    if hp_attn.is_all_pinned(spec):
   229	        pin.spec = None
   230	        with torch.no_grad():
   231	            return hp_attn.forward_all_pinned(model, ids, spec)
   232	    pin.spec = spec
   233	    try:
   234	        with torch.no_grad():
   235	            h = model.model(input_ids=torch.tensor([ids], device=_dev()), use_cache=False).last_hidden_state[0]
   236	    finally:
   237	        pin.spec = None
   238	    return h
   239	
   240	
   387	    try:
   388	        for layer in core.layers:
   389	            try:
   390	                cache = DynamicCache(config=model.config)
   391	            except TypeError:
   392	                cache = DynamicCache()
   393	            h_pi = hidden.index_select(1, pi).contiguous()
   394	            h_pi = _hidden_of(layer(
   395	                h_pi, attention_mask=None, position_ids=pos_pi, past_key_values=cache,
   396	                use_cache=True, position_embeddings=core.rotary_emb(h_pi, pos_pi),
   397	                cache_position=pos_pi[0],
   398	            ))
   399	            hidden[:, pi] = h_pi
   400	            for t in range(T):
   401	                idx = torch.stack((state[t], event[t]))
   402	                h_b = hidden.index_select(1, idx).contiguous()
   403	                h_b = _hidden_of(layer(
   404	                    h_b, attention_mask=mask, position_ids=pos_b,
   405	                    past_key_values=pb_pin._clone(cache), use_cache=True,
   406	                    position_embeddings=core.rotary_emb(h_b, pos_b), cache_position=pos_b[0],
   407	                ))
   408	                hidden[0, state[t]] = h_b[0, 0]
   409	                hidden[0, event[t]] = h_b[0, 1]
   410	    finally:
   411	        for attn, orig in hooks:
   412	            attn.forward = orig
   413	    return core.norm(hidden)[0]

    46	    return tuple((layer, head) for layer in range(n_layers) for head in range(n_heads))

     6	def splice_before(ids, inserts, at):
     7	    """Insert `inserts` immediately before each original index in `at` (a set).
     8	    Returns (new_ids, old_to_new) where old_to_new[i] is the new index of original token i."""
     9	    at = set(at)
    10	    block = list(inserts)
    11	    out, mp = [], {}
    12	    for i, tok in enumerate(ids):
    13	        if i in at:
    14	            out.extend(block)

    20	def requote_positions(n, pi_end, run_start, run_len, W):
    21	    """Original indices before which a fresh Π is inserted.
    22	
    23	    Counting starts at the end of the first Π (`pi_end`) and walks every later token,
    24	    including the rest of the program and the run. A copy is inserted every W of those
    25	    tokens. Insertions that would fall past the run are not made: the lag that matters
    26	    is along the execution, and tokens after the run are not scored.
    27	    """
    28	    if W <= 0:
    29	        raise ValueError(W)
    30	    at, seen = [], 0
    31	    stop = run_start + run_len
    32	    for i in range(pi_end, n):
    33	        if seen == W and i <= stop:
    34	            at.append(i)
    35	            seen = 0
    36	        if i == stop:
    37	            break
    38	        seen += 1
    39	    return at
    40	
    41	
    42	def clock_mask_rows(P):
    43	    """Bool rows (attend = True) for the two block queries over Π + block. Query 0 is the
    44	    state at clock position P; query 1 is the event at P+1. Keys are 0 .. P+1."""
    45	    rows = []
    46	    for q in (P, P + 1):
    47	        rows.append([kv <= q for kv in range(P + 2)])
    48	    return rows
    49	
    50	
    51	def multiset(ids):
    52	    from collections import Counter
    53	    return Counter(ids)
    54	
    55	
    56	def s_value(z_uu, z_uv, z_vu, z_vv):
    57	    """S(n) = (z_u[u] - z_u[v]) - (z_v[u] - z_v[v])."""
    58	    return (z_uu - z_uv) - (z_vu - z_vv)
    59	
    60	
    61	def margin_from_logits(logits, target):
    62	    """Target logit minus the best other logit. `logits` is a sequence of numbers."""
    63	    t = logits[target]
    64	    best = None
    65	    for i, v in enumerate(logits):
    66	        if i == target:
    67	            continue
    68	        if best is None or v > best:
    69	            best = v
    70	    return t - best

   241	def margins_at(model, h, positions, targets, chunk=256):
   242	    import numpy as np
   243	    import torch
   244	    if not positions:
   245	        return np.zeros(0, np.float32), np.zeros(0, np.int32)
   246	    m, am = [], []
   247	    tgt = torch.as_tensor(targets)
   248	    with torch.no_grad():
   249	        for i in range(0, len(positions), chunk):
   250	            idx = torch.tensor(positions[i:i + chunk], device=h.device)
   251	            lg = model.lm_head(h[idx]).float()
   252	            tt = tgt[i:i + chunk].to(lg.device)
   253	            tl = lg.gather(1, tt[:, None])[:, 0]
   254	            other = lg.scatter(1, tt[:, None], float("-inf")).max(1).values
   255	            m.append((tl - other).cpu().numpy())
   256	            am.append(lg.argmax(1).cpu().numpy())
   257	            del lg
   258	    return np.concatenate(m).astype(np.float32), np.concatenate(am).astype(np.int32)

   260	
   261	def _pack(policy, tape, state_m, state_am, state_tgt, read_m, read_am, read_tgt, extra=None):
   262	    import numpy as np
   263	    T = len(state_tgt)
   264	    state_ok = state_am == np.asarray(state_tgt)
   265	    read_ok = read_am == np.asarray(read_tgt) if len(read_tgt) else np.ones(0, dtype=bool)
   266	    first = None
   267	    for k in range(T):
   268	        if len(read_ok) and not read_ok[k]:
   269	            first = {"step": k, "kind": "read"}
   270	            break
   271	        if not state_ok[k]:
   272	            first = {"step": k, "kind": "step"}
   273	            break
   274	    rec = {"policy": policy, "family": tape["family"], "L": tape["L"], "i": tape["i"], "seed": tape["seed"],
   275	           "n_tokens": tape["lay"]["n"], "exact_state": bool(state_ok.all()),
   276	           "exact_read": None if not len(read_ok) else bool(read_ok.all()),
   277	           "first_error": first, "min_margin": float(state_m.min()) if T else None,
   278	           "n_state_wrong": int((~state_ok).sum()), "n_read_wrong": int((~read_ok).sum()) if len(read_ok) else 0}
   279	    if extra:
   280	        rec.update(extra)
   281	    arrays = {"margin_state": state_m, "argmax_state": state_am.astype(np.int32),
   282	              "target_state": np.asarray(state_tgt, np.int32),
   283	              "margin_read": read_m, "argmax_read": read_am.astype(np.int32),
   284	              "target_read": np.asarray(read_tgt, np.int32)}
   285	    return rec, arrays
   286	
   287	
   288	def _margin_1d(lg, target):
   289	    import torch
   290	    x = lg.float()
   291	    other = x.clone()
   292	    other[int(target)] = float("-inf")
   293	    return float(x[int(target)] - other.max()), int(x.argmax())
   294	
   295	
   296	def run_pin_all(model, tape, check_first=8):
   297	    """The clock. The first `check_first` steps are real ticks; if they match the certificate bitwise,
   298	    the rest of the run is the certificate (Theorem P). Otherwise every step is a real tick."""
   299	    import numpy as np
   300	    import pb_pin
   301	    import torch
   302	    lay, geo, ids = tape["lay"], tape["geo"], tape["lay"]["ids"]
   303	    trip = [(r["s"], r["e"], r["t"]) for r in lay["rows"][0]]
   304	    cert, past, logits = pb_pin.certify(model, tape["pi"], trip, keep_logits=True)
   305	    T = lay["T"]
   306	    n_check = min(check_first, T)
   307	    bitwise = 0
   308	    sm, sa, st = [], [], []
   309	
   310	    def take(k, lg):
   311	        target = ids[geo["event_pos"][k] + 1]
   312	        margin, arg = _margin_1d(lg, target)
   313	        sm.append(margin)
   314	        sa.append(arg)
   315	        st.append(target)
   316	
   317	    for k in range(n_check):
   318	        s, e = ids[geo["state_pos"][k]], ids[geo["event_pos"][k]]
   319	        lg = pb_pin.tick(model, past, len(tape["pi"]), s, e)
   320	        bitwise += int(torch.equal(lg, logits[(s, e)]))
   321	        take(k, lg)
   322	        del lg
   323	    use_cert = bitwise == n_check
   324	    for k in range(n_check, T):
   325	        s, e = ids[geo["state_pos"][k]], ids[geo["event_pos"][k]]
   326	        lg = logits[(s, e)] if use_cert else pb_pin.tick(model, past, len(tape["pi"]), s, e)
   327	        take(k, lg)
   328	        if not use_cert:
   329	            del lg
   330	    del past, logits
   331	    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   332	    extra = {"bitwise_checks": n_check, "bitwise_equal": bitwise, "used_certificate": use_cert,
   333	             "rows": len(trip), "certified": cert["pass"]}
   334	    return _pack("pin-all", tape, np.asarray(sm, np.float32), np.asarray(sa, np.int32), st,
   335	                 np.zeros(0, np.float32), np.zeros(0, np.int32), [], extra)
   336	
   337	
   338	def _score_queries(model, h, tape, state_pos, read_pos):
   339	    ids = tape["lay"]["ids"]
   340	    geo = tape["geo"]
   341	    # state_pos / read_pos here are logit positions, already resolved in the sequence that was forwarded
   342	    state_tgt = [ids[geo["event_pos"][k] + 1] for k in range(tape["lay"]["T"])]
   343	    read_tgt = [ids[p] for p in geo["event_pos"]]
   344	    sm, sa = margins_at(model, h, state_pos, state_tgt)
   345	    rm, ra = margins_at(model, h, read_pos, read_tgt)
   346	    return sm, sa, state_tgt, rm, ra, read_tgt
   347	
   348	
   349	def run_full(model, pin, tape, policy="full"):
   350	    h = forward(model, tape["lay"]["ids"], pin, None)
   351	    geo = tape["geo"]
   352	    state_pos = geo["event_pos"]
   353	    read_pos = [p - 1 for p in geo["event_pos"]]
   354	    sm, sa, st, rm, ra, rt = _score_queries(model, h, tape, state_pos, read_pos)
   355	    del h
   356	    return _pack(policy, tape, sm, sa, st, rm, ra, rt, {"n_tokens": tape["lay"]["n"]})
   357	
   358	
   359	def run_pinned_heads(model, pin, tape, pairs, policy):
   360	    import hp_attn
   361	    spec = hp_attn.Spec(pairs, tape["geo"]["pi_pos"], tape["geo"]["state_pos"], tape["geo"]["event_pos"],
   362	                        hp_heads.N_HEADS).to(_dev())
   363	    h = forward(model, tape["lay"]["ids"], pin, spec)
   364	    geo = tape["geo"]
   365	    sm, sa, st, rm, ra, rt = _score_queries(model, h, tape, geo["event_pos"], [p - 1 for p in geo["event_pos"]])
   366	    del h
   367	    return _pack(policy, tape, sm, sa, st, rm, ra, rt)
   368	
   369	
   370	def run_requote(model, pin, tape, W):
   371	    ids, mp, at = requote_of(tape, W)
   372	    if len(ids) > limit():
   373	        raise RuntimeError(f"requote-{W} length {len(ids)} exceeds {limit()}")
   374	    h = forward(model, ids, pin, None)
   375	    geo = tape["geo"]
   376	    state_pos = [mp[geo["event_pos"][k] + 1] - 1 for k in range(tape["lay"]["T"])]
   377	    read_pos = [mp[p] - 1 for p in geo["event_pos"]]
   378	    sm, sa, st, rm, ra, rt = _score_queries(model, h, tape, state_pos, read_pos)
   379	    del h
   380	    return _pack(f"requote-{W}", tape, sm, sa, st, rm, ra, rt,
   381	                 {"n_tokens": len(ids), "n_insertions": len(at)})
   382	
   383	
   384	def run_policy(model, tok, pin, tape, policy, check_first=8):
   385	    del tok
   386	    if policy == "pin-all":
   387	        return run_pin_all(model, tape, check_first=check_first)
   388	    if policy == "full":
   389	        return run_full(model, pin, tape)
   390	    if policy == "pin-committee":
   391	        return run_pinned_heads(model, pin, tape, hp_heads.COMMITTEE, policy)
   392	    if policy == "pin-random":
   393	        return run_pinned_heads(model, pin, tape, hp_heads.RANDOM, policy)
   394	    if policy.startswith("requote-"):
   395	        return run_requote(model, pin, tape, int(policy.split("-", 1)[1]))
   396	    raise ValueError(policy)

   399	def key_of(rec):
   400	    return f"{rec['policy']}|{rec['family']}|L{rec['L']}|{rec['i']}"

   402	
   403	def e0_margin(z_t, top, top_i, target):
   404	    """Target minus the best other, from the target logit and the top two (value, index) pairs."""
   405	    if int(top_i[0]) != int(target):
   406	        return float(z_t) - float(top[0])
   407	    return float(top[0]) - float(top[1])
   408	
   409	
   410	def run_e0(model, tok, seed=E0_SEED, n_tables=E0_TABLES, delays=E0_DELAYS):
   411	    """4 perm tables, both destinations, both placements. Returns one record per (table, delay, placement)."""
   412	    import pb_pin
   413	    import torch
   414	    pool = pb_pin.name_pool(tok)
   415	    rng = random.Random(seed)
   416	    tables = [pb_pin.draw_table(rng, pool["train"], n=4, a=3, shape="perm") for _ in range(n_tables)]
   417	    rows = []
   418	    for ti, table in enumerate(tables):
   419	        for delay in delays:
   420	            built = hp_e0.sequences(table, pool["id_of"], pool["nl"], delay, f"{seed}|t{ti}")
   421	            if built["trace_len"] != delay:
   422	                raise RuntimeError("delay is not the trace length")
   423	            got, n_tokens = {}, {}
   424	            for (name, reloc), seq in built["seqs"].items():
   425	                ids = seq["ids"]
   426	                if len(ids) > limit():
   427	                    raise RuntimeError(f"E0 length {len(ids)} exceeds {limit()}")
   428	                with torch.no_grad():
   429	                    h = model.model(input_ids=torch.tensor([ids], device=_dev()),
   430	                                    use_cache=False).last_hidden_state[0]
   431	                    lg = model.lm_head(h[-1:]).float()[0]
   432	                topv, topi = torch.topk(lg, 2)
   433	                got[(name, reloc)] = {"z_u": float(lg[built["u_id"]]), "z_v": float(lg[built["v_id"]]),
   434	                                      "top": [float(topv[0]), float(topv[1])],
   435	                                      "top_i": [int(topi[0]), int(topi[1])]}
   436	                n_tokens[(name, reloc)] = len(ids)
   437	                del h, lg
   438	            for reloc in (False, True):
   439	                if n_tokens[("u", reloc)] != n_tokens[("v", reloc)]:
   440	                    raise RuntimeError("u and v programs differ in length")
   441	                if n_tokens[("u", False)] != n_tokens[("u", True)]:
   442	                    raise RuntimeError("relocation changed the length")
   443	                u, v = got[("u", reloc)], got[("v", reloc)]
   444	                zuu, zuv, zvu, zvv = u["z_u"], u["z_v"], v["z_u"], v["z_v"]
   445	                rows.append({
   446	                    "table": ti, "delay": int(delay), "relocate": bool(reloc), "seed": seed,
   447	                    "S": hp_seq.s_value(zuu, zuv, zvu, zvv),
   448	                    "margin_u": e0_margin(zuu, u["top"], u["top_i"], built["u_id"]),
   449	                    "margin_v": e0_margin(zvv, v["top"], v["top_i"], built["v_id"]),
   450	                    "z_uu": zuu, "z_uv": zuv, "z_vu": zvu, "z_vv": zvv,
   451	                    "top_u": u["top"], "top_u_i": u["top_i"], "top_v": v["top"], "top_v_i": v["top_i"],
   452	                    "u_id": built["u_id"], "v_id": built["v_id"],
   453	                    "s": built["s"], "e": built["e"], "u": built["u"], "v": built["v"],
   454	                    "n_tokens": n_tokens[("u", reloc)], "n_states": len(table["states"]),
   455	                    "n_events": len(table["events"]), "shape": table["shape"],
   456	                })
   457	    return rows
    86	def identity(model, tok, pin, tape):
    87	    """All heads must match the clock bitwise. No heads must match a plain forward bitwise."""
    88	    import torch
    89	    import pb_pin
    90	    pin.spec = None
    91	    ids, geo, pi = tape["lay"]["ids"], tape["geo"], tape["pi"]
    92	    P, T = len(pi), tape["lay"]["T"]
    93	    dev = H._dev()
    94	    layer0 = model.model.layers[0].self_attn
    95	
    96	    store, orig = _hook_first(layer0)
    97	    try:
    98	        past = pb_pin.prefill(model, pi)
    99	    finally:
   100	        _restore(layer0, orig)
   101	    prefill_mask = store.get("mask", "missing")
   102	
   103	    # every layer's block input on the first tick, and every tick's final hidden state
   104	    tick_layers, hooks = {}, []
   105	    for layer in model.model.layers:
   106	        attn = layer.self_attn
   107	        box, orig = {}, attn.forward
   108	
   109	        def wrapped(hidden_states, position_embeddings, attention_mask, past_key_values=None, _attn=attn, _orig=orig, _box=box, **kw):
   110	            if "h" not in _box:
   111	                _box["h"] = hidden_states.detach().clone()
   112	                _box["mask"] = None if attention_mask is None else attention_mask.detach().clone()
   113	            out = _orig(hidden_states=hidden_states, position_embeddings=position_embeddings,
   114	                        attention_mask=attention_mask, past_key_values=past_key_values, **kw)
   115	            if "y" not in _box:
   116	                _box["y"] = out[0].detach().clone()
   117	            return out
   118	
   119	        attn.forward = wrapped
   120	        hooks.append((attn, orig, box))
   121	    norm = model.model.norm
   122	    norm_orig, finals = norm.forward, []
   123	
   124	    def norm_wrapped(x, _orig=norm_orig):
   125	        y = _orig(x)
   126	        finals.append(y.detach().clone())
   127	        return y
   128	
   129	    norm.forward = norm_wrapped
   130	    ticks = []
   131	    try:
   132	        for k in range(T):
   133	            s, e = ids[geo["state_pos"][k]], ids[geo["event_pos"][k]]
   134	            ticks.append(pb_pin.tick(model, past, P, s, e).detach().clone())
   135	    finally:
   136	        norm.forward = norm_orig
   137	        for attn, orig, box in hooks:
   138	            attn.forward = orig
   139	            tick_layers[attn.layer_idx] = box.get("h")
   140	    layer0_box = next(box for attn, _orig, box in hooks if attn.layer_idx == 0)
   141	    tick_h, tick_mask, tick_y = layer0_box.get("h"), layer0_box.get("mask"), layer0_box.get("y")
   142	    del past
   143	
   144	    spec = hp_attn.Spec(hp_heads.all_heads(), geo["pi_pos"], geo["state_pos"], geo["event_pos"],
   145	                        hp_heads.N_HEADS, debug=True).to(dev)
   146	    h = H.forward(model, ids, pin, spec)
   147	    # the clock's logit is lm_head of the two block positions together; a different batch is a different bf16 product
   148	    step_h_abs, step_logit_abs = [], []
   149	    hidden_equal, logit_equal = True, True
   150	    with torch.no_grad():
   151	        for k in range(T):
   152	            ref = finals[k][0, -1]
   153	            got_h = h[geo["event_pos"][k]]
   154	            hidden_equal = hidden_equal and bool(torch.equal(got_h, ref))
   155	            step_h_abs.append(_max_abs(got_h, ref))
   156	            pair = torch.stack([h[geo["state_pos"][k]], got_h])[None]
   157	            lg = model.lm_head(pair)[0, -1]
   158	            logit_equal = logit_equal and bool(torch.equal(lg, ticks[k]))
   159	            step_logit_abs.append(_max_abs(lg, ticks[k]))
   160	    logit_abs = max(step_logit_abs) if step_logit_abs else None
   161	    long_h = None if spec.debug is None else spec.debug.get("long_h0")
   162	    input_equal = long_h is not None and tick_h is not None and torch.equal(long_h, tick_h)
   163	    input_abs = None if long_h is None or tick_h is None else _max_abs(long_h, tick_h)
   164	    block = None if spec.debug is None else spec.debug.get("long_block0")
   165	    y_direct = None if spec.debug is None else spec.debug.get("layer0_y")
   166	    block_equal, block_abs = False, None
   167	    if y_direct is not None and tick_y is not None:
   168	        block_equal = bool(torch.equal(y_direct, tick_y))
   169	        block_abs = _max_abs(y_direct, tick_y)
   170	    elif block is not None and tick_y is not None:
   171	        custom_y = layer0.o_proj(block.reshape(1, 2, -1).contiguous())
   172	        block_equal = bool(torch.equal(custom_y, tick_y))
   173	        block_abs = _max_abs(custom_y, tick_y)
   174	    ours = hp_attn.clock_mask(torch, P, dev)
   175	    mask_equal = tick_mask is not None and torch.equal(ours, tick_mask)
   176	    mask_abs = None
   177	    if tick_mask is not None and tick_mask.shape == ours.shape and tick_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
   178	        mask_abs = int((ours != tick_mask).sum())
   179	
   180	    h_full = H.forward(model, ids, pin, None)
   181	    spec0 = hp_attn.Spec([], geo["pi_pos"], geo["state_pos"], geo["event_pos"], hp_heads.N_HEADS).to(dev)
   182	    h_none = H.forward(model, ids, pin, spec0)
   183	    none_equal = bool(torch.equal(h_full, h_none))
   184	    none_abs = _max_abs(h_full, h_none)
   185	
   186	    spec_std = hp_attn.Spec([], geo["pi_pos"], geo["state_pos"], geo["event_pos"], hp_heads.N_HEADS,
   187	                            debug=True, std_only=True).to(dev)
   188	    H.forward(model, ids, pin, spec_std)
   189	    std_equal = bool(spec_std.debug and spec_std.debug.get("std_equal"))
   190	    std_max = None if spec_std.debug is None else spec_std.debug.get("std_max")
   191	
   192	    cfg = model.config
   193	    layer_types = list(getattr(cfg, "layer_types", []) or [])
   194	    prefill_none = prefill_mask is None
   195	    block_h = {} if spec.debug is None else spec.debug.get("block_h", {})
   196	    layer_abs, first_layer = [], None
   197	    for i in range(int(cfg.num_hidden_layers)):
   198	        a, b = block_h.get(i), tick_layers.get(i)
   199	        if a is None or b is None:
   200	            layer_abs.append(None)
   201	            continue
   202	        diff = _max_abs(a, b)
   203	        layer_abs.append(diff)
   204	        if diff != 0.0 and first_layer is None:
   205	            first_layer = i
   206	    passed = bool(logit_equal and hidden_equal and input_equal and block_equal and mask_equal and none_equal
   207	                  and std_equal and prefill_none)
   208	    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   209	    return {"pass": passed, "T": T, "P": P, "n_tokens": len(ids),
   210	            "all_heads_logits_equal": bool(logit_equal), "all_heads_max_abs": logit_abs,
   211	            "all_heads_hidden_equal": bool(hidden_equal), "step_hidden_max_abs": step_h_abs,
   212	            "step_logit_max_abs": step_logit_abs, "first_layer_diff": first_layer, "layer_input_max_abs": layer_abs,
   213	            "layer0_input_equal": bool(input_equal), "layer0_input_max_abs": input_abs,
   214	            "layer0_block_equal": bool(block_equal), "layer0_block_max_abs": block_abs,
   215	            "mask_equal": bool(mask_equal), "mask_mismatches": mask_abs,
   216	            "prefill_mask_none": bool(prefill_none),
   217	            "no_heads_hidden_equal": bool(none_equal), "no_heads_max_abs": none_abs,
   218	            "std_equal": std_equal, "std_max": std_max,
   219	            "n_layers": int(cfg.num_hidden_layers), "n_heads": int(cfg.num_attention_heads),
   220	            "n_kv": int(cfg.num_key_value_heads),
   221	            "layer_types": sorted(set(layer_types)),
   222	            "use_sliding_window": bool(getattr(cfg, "use_sliding_window", False)),
   223	            "tick_mask_shape": None if tick_mask is None else list(tick_mask.shape),

    59	def _tape_positions(tok, prompt, question, lit):
    60	    """Token indices (in the encoded prompt) of the tape's events, by the clock's own walk (pb_lit.walk)."""
    61	    q0 = prompt.find(question)
    62	    assert q0 >= 0
    63	    a, b = pb_lit.find_span(question, *lit["anchors"])
    64	    a, b = a + q0, b + q0
    65	    e = tok.encode(prompt, add_special_tokens=False)
    66	    names = set(lit["events"])
    67	    out = []
    68	    for i, (s, t) in enumerate(e.offsets):
    69	        raw = prompt[s:t]
    70	        s2, t2 = s + len(raw) - len(raw.lstrip()), t - (len(raw) - len(raw.rstrip()))
    71	        if t2 <= a or s2 >= b:
    72	            continue
    73	        if prompt[s2:t2] in names:
    74	            out.append(i)
    75	    return out

    78	def layout(tok, ex, lit):
    79	    """ids of the whole plain-mode sequence and the index of every part. Run step k (0-based) has its event at
    80	    run_start + k*stride and register r's value at run_start + k*stride + 1 + r."""
    81	    prompt = enc(tok, ex["prompt"])
    82	    prog = pb_lit.program_ids(tok, lit)
    83	    # Π positions, rebuilt piece by piece exactly as pb_lit.program_ids builds the ids
    84	    nl = enc(tok, "\n")
    85	    import json as _json
    86	    head = enc(tok, f"<program>\ntask: {lit['task']}\ninput: {lit['input']}\n")
    87	    before, after = lit["anchors"]
    88	    head += enc(tok, "tape: after " + _json.dumps(before, ensure_ascii=False) + " until "
    89	                + _json.dumps(after, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    90	    pos = len(prompt) + len(head)
    91	    rows, rebuilt = [], list(head)
    92	    for r in lit["registers"]:
    93	        reg_head = enc(tok, f"register {r['name']}: start") + [pb_lit.name_id(tok, r["start"])] + nl
    94	        rebuilt += reg_head
    95	        pos += len(reg_head)
    96	        rr = []
    97	        for s, e, t in r["rows"]:
    98	            ids = [pb_lit.name_id(tok, s), pb_lit.name_id(tok, e), pb_lit.name_id(tok, t)]
    99	            rr.append({"pos": pos, "s": ids[0], "e": ids[1], "t": ids[2]})
   100	            rebuilt += ids + nl
   101	            pos += 4
   102	        rows.append(rr)
   103	    rebuilt += enc(tok, f"answer: {lit['answer_line']}\n</program>\n<exec/>")
   104	    assert rebuilt == prog, "program layout does not match pb_lit.program_ids"
   105	    opener = enc(tok, "\n<run>\n")
   106	    run = pb_lit.run_ids(tok, lit)
   107	    closer = enc(tok, "\n</run>\n")
   108	    ids = prompt + prog + opener + run + closer
   109	    R = len(lit["registers"])
   110	    tape_pos = _tape_positions(tok, ex["prompt"], ex["question"], lit)
   111	    reg_states = [sorted({pb_lit.name_id(tok, v) for v in r["name_of"].values()}) for r in lit["registers"]]
   112	    events = sorted({pb_lit.name_id(tok, e) for e in lit["events"]})
   113	    table = [{(x["s"], x["e"]): x["t"] for x in rr} for rr in rows]
   114	    starts = [pb_lit.name_id(tok, r["start"]) for r in lit["registers"]]
   115	    return {"ids": ids, "prompt_len": len(prompt), "prog_start": len(prompt), "prog_len": len(prog),
   116	            "run_start": len(prompt) + len(prog) + len(opener), "T": len(lit["tape"]), "R": R, "stride": 1 + R,
   117	            "rows": rows, "tape_pos": tape_pos, "states": reg_states, "events": events, "table": table,
   118	            "starts": starts, "n": len(ids)}
   119	
   120	
   121	def run_tokens(lay):
   122	    """(index, kind, step, register) for every run token; kind 'e' (read) or 's' (step)."""
   123	    out = []
   124	    for k in range(lay["T"]):
   125	        p = lay["run_start"] + k * lay["stride"]
   126	        out.append((p, "e", k, -1))
   127	        for r in range(lay["R"]):
   128	            out.append((p + 1 + r, "s", k, r))
   129	    return out

   132	def examples(tok, families, lengths, n, seed, split="train", max_tokens=CONTEXT - 64):
   133	    """n examples per (family, length), drawn as pb_loop draws its gate cells; skipped if unpinnable or over context.
   134	    Each cell has its own generator, seeded by (seed, family, length), so a cell's examples do not depend on which
   135	    other cells a job draws."""
   136	    out, skipped = [], {}
   137	    for fam in families:
   138	        for L in lengths:
   139	            rng = random.Random(f"{seed}|{fam}|{L}")
   140	            made, tries = 0, 0
   141	            while made < n and tries < 20 * n:
   142	                tries += 1
   143	                ex = family_example(rng, fam, L=L, split=split)
   144	                lit, why = pb_lit.literal(ex, tok)
   145	                if lit is None:
   146	                    skipped[f"{fam}|L{L}"] = skipped.get(f"{fam}|L{L}", 0) + 1
   147	                    continue
   148	                lay = layout(tok, ex, lit)
   149	                if lay["n"] > max_tokens:
   150	                    skipped[f"{fam}|L{L}|over_context"] = skipped.get(f"{fam}|L{L}|over_context", 0) + 1
   151	                    continue
   152	                assert len(lay["tape_pos"]) == lay["T"], (fam, L, len(lay["tape_pos"]), lay["T"])
   153	                out.append({"family": fam, "L": L, "cell": f"{fam}|L{L}", "ex": ex, "lit": lit, "lay": lay})
   154	                made += 1
   155	    return out, skipped

   160	def load_model(adapter=SAVANT, model=MODEL):
   161	    """bf16 on the GPU, adapter merged in memory into a fresh copy (never a cached merge: PROMPTBOX §3.10)."""
   162	    import torch
   163	    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM
   164	    m = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(model, dtype=torch.bfloat16, attn_implementation="sdpa").to(_dev())
   165	    if adapter:
   166	        from peft import PeftModel
   167	        m = PeftModel.from_pretrained(m, adapter).merge_and_unload()
   168	    m.eval()
   169	    for p in m.parameters():
   170	        p.requires_grad_(False)
   171	    return m

   174	def hidden(model, ids=None, embeds=None):
   175	    """Final-normed hidden states (n, d) of one causal sequence."""
   176	    import torch
   177	    kw = {"input_ids": torch.tensor([ids], device=_dev())} if embeds is None else {"inputs_embeds": embeds[None]}
   178	    return model.model(**kw, use_cache=False).last_hidden_state[0]

   180	
   170	def literal(ex, tok):
   171	    """The literal-table program for a family example, or None with a reason."""
   172	    try:
   173	        prog = parse_program(ex["prog_text"])
   174	    except ProgramError as exc:
   175	        return None, f"program: {exc}"
   176	    if prog.other:
   177	        return None, "has an 'other' event class"
   178	    for r in prog.registers:
   179	        if deps(prog, r) not in ([r.name], []):
   180	            return None, f"register {r.name} reads another register"
   181	    events = prog.event_classes()
   182	    if not all(name_id(tok, e) for e in events) or len({name_id(tok, e) for e in events}) != len(events):
   183	        return None, "an event has no one-token name"
   184	    regs = []
   185	    for r in prog.registers:
   186	        names = _value_names(tok, r)
   187	        if names is None:
   188	            return None, f"register {r.name} has values without one-token names"
   189	        if len(r.values) * len(events) > MAX_ROWS:
   190	            return None, f"register {r.name} has {len(r.values) * len(events)} rows"
   191	        name_of = dict(zip(r.values, names))
   192	        rows = []
   193	        for v in r.values:
   194	            for e in events:
   195	                state = dict(prog.start)
   196	                state[r.name] = v
   197	                rows.append((name_of[v], e, name_of[step_reg(prog, r, state, e)]))
   198	        regs.append({"name": r.name, "start": name_of[prog.start[r.name]], "rows": rows, "name_of": name_of})
   199	    items = [prog.classify(it) for it in ex["items"]]
   200	    anc = anchors(ex["question"], ex["items"])
   201	    if anc is None:
   202	        return None, "items are not a separator-delimited list in the question"
   203	    span = find_span(ex["question"], *anc)
   204	    tape, why = walk(tok, ex["question"], span, events) if span else (None, "anchors not found")
   205	    if tape != [str(i) for i in items]:
   206	        return None, f"the clock would misread the tape ({why})"
   207	    steps, final = run_program(prog, ex["items"])
   208	    task = re.search(r"^task: (.*)$", ex["prog_text"], re.M).group(1)
   209	    inp = re.search(r"^input: (.*)$", ex["prog_text"], re.M).group(1)
   210	    ans = re.search(r"^answer: (.*)$", ex["prog_text"], re.M).group(1)
   211	    return {"task": task, "input": inp, "answer_line": ans, "events": events, "registers": regs, "tape": tape,
   212	            "anchors": anc, "final": {r["name"]: r["name_of"][final[r["name"]]] for r in regs},
   213	            "trace": [{r["name"]: r["name_of"][st[r["name"]]] for r in regs} for _, _, st in steps],
   214	            "answer": ex["answer"]}, None
   215
