   219	def _enc(tok, text):
   220	    return list(tok.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False).ids)
   221	
   222	
   223	def program_ids(tok, lit):
   224	    nl = _enc(tok, "\n")
   225	    ids = _enc(tok, f"<program>\ntask: {lit['task']}\ninput: {lit['input']}\n")
   226	    before, after = lit["anchors"]
   227	    ids += _enc(tok, "tape: after " + json.dumps(before, ensure_ascii=False) + " until "
   228	                + json.dumps(after, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
   229	    for r in lit["registers"]:
   230	        ids += _enc(tok, f"register {r['name']}: start") + [name_id(tok, r["start"])] + nl
   231	        for s, e, t in r["rows"]:
   232	            ids += [name_id(tok, s), name_id(tok, e), name_id(tok, t)] + nl
   233	    ids += _enc(tok, f"answer: {lit['answer_line']}\n</program>\n<exec/>")
   234	    return ids
   235	
   236	
   237	def final_ids(tok, lit, final=None):
   238	    final = final or lit["final"]
   239	    ids = _enc(tok, "\n<final>")
   240	    for r in lit["registers"]:
   241	        ids += _enc(tok, f" {r['name']}=") + [name_id(tok, final[r["name"]])]
   242	    return ids + _enc(tok, " </final>\n")
   243	
   244	
   245	def run_ids(tok, lit):
   246	    ids = []
   247	    for ev, st in zip(lit["tape"], lit["trace"]):
   248	        ids.append(name_id(tok, ev))
   249	        ids += [name_id(tok, st[r["name"]]) for r in lit["registers"]]
   250	    return ids
   251	
   252	
   253	def example_ids(tok, ex, lit, mode, im_end):
   254	    """(ids, labels) for prompt + response. Harness-written spans (`<final>…</final>`, `<run>` opener) are inputs,
   255	    not targets."""
   256	    prompt = _enc(tok, ex["prompt"])
   257	    prog = program_ids(tok, lit)
   258	    ids, labels = prompt + prog, [-100] * len(prompt) + prog
   259	    if mode == "pinned":
   260	        fin = final_ids(tok, lit)
   261	        ids += fin
   262	        labels += [-100] * len(fin)
   263	    else:
   264	        opener = _enc(tok, "\n<run>\n")
   265	        run = run_ids(tok, lit) + _enc(tok, "\n</run>\n")
   266	        ids += opener + run
   267	        labels += [-100] * len(opener) + run
   268	    ans = _enc(tok, lit["answer"]) + [im_end]
   269	    return ids + ans, labels + ans
   209	def prefill(model, pi_ids):
   210	    import torch
   211	    ids = torch.tensor([pi_ids], device=model.device)
   212	    pos = torch.arange(len(pi_ids), device=model.device)[None]
   213	    with torch.no_grad():
   214	        return model(input_ids=ids, position_ids=pos, use_cache=True).past_key_values
   215	
   216	
   217	def _clone(past):
   218	    import copy
   219	    import torch
   220	    if hasattr(past, "layers"):
   221	        new = copy.copy(past)
   222	        new.layers = []
   223	        for layer in past.layers:
   224	            c = copy.copy(layer)
   225	            for attr in ("keys", "values"):
   226	                v = getattr(c, attr, None)
   227	                if torch.is_tensor(v):
   228	                    setattr(c, attr, v.clone())
   229	            new.layers.append(c)
   230	        return new
   231	    if hasattr(past, "key_cache"):
   232	        new = copy.copy(past)
   233	        new.key_cache = [t.clone() for t in past.key_cache]
   234	        new.value_cache = [t.clone() for t in past.value_cache]
   235	        return new
   236	    return tuple(tuple(t.clone() for t in layer) for layer in past)
   237	
   238	
   239	def tick(model, past, P, s_id, e_id):
   240	    """One tick: the block [s][e] at positions P, P+1 over a copy of Π's cache. Returns the full-vocabulary
   241	    logits at the event position (the copy is discarded, so Π's cache is never written)."""
   242	    import torch
   243	    pos = torch.tensor([[P, P + 1]], device=model.device)
   244	    with torch.no_grad():
   245	        out = model(input_ids=torch.tensor([[s_id, e_id]], device=model.device), position_ids=pos,
   246	                    cache_position=pos[0], past_key_values=_clone(past), use_cache=True)
   247	    return out.logits[0, -1]
   248	
   249	
   250	def top2(logits):
   251	    import torch
   252	    v, i = torch.topk(logits.float(), 2)
   253	    return int(i[0]), float(v[0] - v[1])
   254	
   255	
   256	def certify(model, pi_ids, rows, keep_logits=False):
   257	    """Every row of S × B, one batch-1 tick each over the same cached Π. Passes iff every argmax (full
   258	    vocabulary) is the row's destination id with a strictly positive margin."""
   259	    past = prefill(model, pi_ids)
   260	    P = len(pi_ids)
   261	    out, logits = [], {}
   262	    for s, e, t in rows:
   263	        lg = tick(model, past, P, s, e)
   264	        arg, margin = top2(lg)
   265	        out.append({"s": s, "e": e, "t": t, "argmax": arg, "margin": round(margin, 4),
   266	                    "ok": arg == t and margin > 0})
   267	        if keep_logits:
   268	            logits[(s, e)] = lg.clone()
   269	    passed = all(r["ok"] for r in out)
   270	    return {"pass": passed, "rows": out, "min_margin": min(r["margin"] for r in out) if out else None,
   271	            "rows_wrong": sum(not r["ok"] for r in out)}, past, logits
   272	
   273	
   274	def run(model, past, P, s0, tape, cert_logits=None):
   275	    """The clock: s_i = argmax M(Π; [s_{i-1}][e_i]). With certified logits, also checks each tick's logits are
     1	"""E0: delayed first use of one row, and the relocation control.
     2	
     3	A table is a pb_pin table. Two programs differ only in row r = (states[0], events[-1]):
     4	its destination is u (the drawn one) or v (the next state in the list). The trace uses
     5	only the other events, so it never queries r and is identical for both programs. Delay
     6	is the trace length in tokens (event, state pairs). Relocation moves the single Π block
     7	to sit immediately before the query [s*][e*], keeping the token multiset and the length.
     8	"""
     9	
    10	from __future__ import annotations
    11	
    12	import random
    13	
    14	import hp_seq
    15	
    16	
    17	def row_r(table):
    18	    s, e = table["states"][0], table["events"][-1]
    19	    u = next(t for ss, ee, t in table["rows"] if ss == s and ee == e)
    20	    v = next(x for x in table["states"] if x != u)
    21	    return s, e, u, v
    22	
    23	
    24	def pi_ids(table, id_of, nl, dest):
    25	    """dest is (s, e, t) overriding that row, or None."""
    26	    over = None if dest is None else (dest[0], dest[1])
    27	    ids = []
    28	    for s, e, t in table["rows"]:
    29	        if over is not None and (s, e) == over:
    30	            t = dest[2]
    31	        ids += [id_of[s], id_of[e], id_of[t], nl]
    32	    return ids
    33	
    34	
    35	def trace_ids(table, id_of, delay, rng):
    36	    """`delay` tokens, even, of a correct trace that never uses events[-1]."""
    37	    if delay % 2 or delay <= 0:
    38	        raise ValueError(delay)
    39	    delta = {(s, e): t for s, e, t in table["rows"]}
    40	    events = table["events"][:-1]
    41	    s = table["states"][1]
    42	    out = []
    43	    for _ in range(delay // 2):
    44	        e = rng.choice(events)
    45	        t = delta[(s, e)]
    46	        out += [id_of[e], id_of[t]]
    47	        s = t
    48	    return out
    49	
    50	
    51	def pair_ids(table, id_of, nl, delay, relocate, dest, trace):
    52	    """One sequence. relocate False: Π, trace, query. True: trace, Π, query."""
    53	    s, e, u, v = row_r(table)
    54	    if dest not in (u, v):
    55	        raise ValueError(dest)
    56	    pi = pi_ids(table, id_of, nl, (s, e, dest))
    57	    query = [id_of[s], id_of[e]]
    58	    body = (trace + pi) if relocate else (pi + trace)
    59	    return body + query, pi, query
    60	
    61	
    62	def sequences(table, id_of, nl, delay, seed):
    63	    """Both destinations and both placements. Trace rng is seeded by (seed, delay) only,
    64	    so the trace does not depend on the destination or the placement."""
    65	    s, e, u, v = row_r(table)
    66	    tr = trace_ids(table, id_of, delay, random.Random(f"{seed}|{delay}|{s}|{e}"))
    67	    out = {}
    68	    for dest, name in ((u, "u"), (v, "v")):
    69	        for relocate in (False, True):
    70	            ids, pi, query = pair_ids(table, id_of, nl, delay, relocate, dest, tr)
    71	            out[(name, relocate)] = {"ids": ids, "pi_len": len(pi), "query": query}
    72	    far_u, near_u = out[("u", False)]["ids"], out[("u", True)]["ids"]
    73	    if hp_seq.multiset(far_u) != hp_seq.multiset(near_u):
    74	        raise RuntimeError("relocation changed token counts")
    75	    if len(far_u) != len(near_u):
    76	        raise RuntimeError("relocation changed length")
    77	    # the two programs differ in exactly one token inside Π; the trace and the query match
    78	    fu, fv = out[("u", False)]["ids"], out[("v", False)]["ids"]
    79	    diffs = [i for i, (a, b) in enumerate(zip(fu, fv)) if a != b]
    80	    if len(diffs) != 1:
    81	        raise RuntimeError(f"programs differ in {len(diffs)} tokens")
    82	    return {"s": s, "e": e, "u": u, "v": v, "u_id": id_of[u], "v_id": id_of[v], "trace_len": len(tr),
    83	            "seqs": out}
    84	
    85	
    86	def components(lg_u, lg_v, u_id, v_id):
    87	    """Pull the four logits and both full-vocabulary margins off one pair of forwards."""
    88	    zuu, zuv = float(lg_u[u_id]), float(lg_u[v_id])
    89	    zvu, zvv = float(lg_v[u_id]), float(lg_v[v_id])
    90	    return {"z_uu": zuu, "z_uv": zuv, "z_vu": zvu, "z_vv": zvv,
    91	            "S": hp_seq.s_value(zuu, zuv, zvu, zvv),
    92	            "margin_u": hp_seq.margin_from_logits(lg_u, u_id),
    93	            "margin_v": hp_seq.margin_from_logits(lg_v, v_id)}
   293	def _load(adapter):
   294	    import br_core as C
   295	    check_adapter(adapter)
   296	    t0 = time.time()
   297	    tok = C.tokenizer()
   298	    model = C.load_model(adapter)
   299	    if model.config.num_hidden_layers != hp_heads.N_LAYERS or model.config.num_attention_heads != hp_heads.N_HEADS:
   300	        raise SystemExit("model head count does not match the committee table")
   301	    log(loaded=round(time.time() - t0, 1))
   302	    return tok, model, C.host()
   303	
   304	
   305	def mode_smoke(a):
   306	    import br_core as C
   307	    hp_heads.check()
   308	    check_adapter(a.adapter)
   309	    tok = C.tokenizer()
   310	    t0 = time.time()
   311	    metas, tapes = H.build_corpus(tok)
   312	    cens = census(tapes)
   313	    cens["tables"] = [json_meta(m) for m in metas]
   314	    cens["seconds"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)
   315	    atomic(pathlib.Path(a.out) / "census.json", cens)
   316	    log(census_n=cens["n"], max_tokens=cens["max_tokens"], over=len(cens["over"]), seconds=cens["seconds"])
   317	    if cens["over"]:
   318	        raise SystemExit(3)
   319	    timing_tape = next(t for t in tapes if t["family"] == "parity" and t["L"] == 1024 and t["i"] == 0)
   320	    meta_p = next(m for m in metas if m["family"] == "parity")
   321	    del tapes
   322	    tok, model, host = _load(a.adapter)
   323	    # tokenizer() was already called; _load calls it again. That is cheap. The first tok is the same cache.
   324	    pin = hp_attn.Pin(model)
   325	    tape = H.smoke_tape(tok, meta_p, 8)
   326	    ident = identity(model, tok, pin, tape)
   327	    atomic(pathlib.Path(a.out) / "identity.json", ident)
   328	    log(identity_pass=ident["pass"], logits=ident["all_heads_logits_equal"], none=ident["no_heads_hidden_equal"],
   329	        std=ident["std_equal"], mask=ident["mask_equal"])
   330	    if not ident["pass"]:
   331	        raise SystemExit(2)
   332	    t1 = time.perf_counter()
   333	    H.run_policy(model, tok, pin, timing_tape, "full")
   334	    full_s = round(time.perf_counter() - t1, 3)
   335	    t1 = time.perf_counter()
   336	    H.run_policy(model, tok, pin, timing_tape, "pin-committee")
   337	    comm_s = round(time.perf_counter() - t1, 3)
   338	    atomic(pathlib.Path(a.out) / "smoke.json",
   339	           {"host": host, "adapter": a.adapter, "identity": ident, "census_max_tokens": cens["max_tokens"],
   340	            "census_n": cens["n"], "census_over": len(cens["over"]),
   341	            "time_full_1024": full_s, "time_pin_committee_1024": comm_s,
   342	            "tables": [{k: m[k] for k in ("family", "attempt", "fmt", "phrasing", "rows")} for m in metas]})
   343	    log(time_full_1024=full_s, time_pin_committee_1024=comm_s)
   344	
   345	
   346	def _recheck(model, tok, pin, meta, out):
   347	    tape = H.smoke_tape(tok, meta, 8)
   348	    ident = identity(model, tok, pin, tape)
   349	    atomic(pathlib.Path(out) / "identity.json", ident)
   350	    log(identity_pass=ident["pass"])
   351	    if not ident["pass"]:
   352	        raise SystemExit(2)
   353	
   354	
   355	def mode_count(a):
   356	    import br_core as C
   357	    hp_heads.check()
   358	    check_adapter(a.adapter)
   359	    families = tuple(a.families.split(","))
   360	    lengths = tuple(int(x) for x in a.lengths.split(","))
   361	    policies = tuple(a.policies.split(","))
   362	    out = pathlib.Path(a.resume) if a.resume else pathlib.Path(a.out)
   363	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   364	    t0 = time.time()
   365	    tok = C.tokenizer()
   366	    metas, tapes = H.build_corpus(tok, families, lengths, a.n)
   367	    cens = census(tapes)
   368	    atomic(out / "census.json", {**cens, "tables": [json_meta(m) for m in metas]})
   369	    log(corpus=len(tapes), max_tokens=cens["max_tokens"], over=len(cens["over"]), seconds=round(time.time() - t0, 1))
   370	    if cens["over"]:
   371	        raise SystemExit(3)
   372	    tok, model, host = _load(a.adapter)
   373	    pin = hp_attn.Pin(model)
   374	    meta_p = next(m for m in metas if m["family"] == "parity")
   375	    _recheck(model, tok, pin, meta_p, out)
   376	    atomic(out / "run.json",
   377	           {"host": host, "seed": H.SEED, "families": list(families), "lengths": list(lengths),
   378	            "n": a.n, "policies": list(policies), "adapter": a.adapter, "status": "running"})
   379	    (out / "runs").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
   380	    done = _done(out / "runs.jsonl")
   381	    import numpy as np
   382	    n_done = 0
   383	    for policy in policies:
   384	        for tape in tapes:
   385	            rec_key = f"{policy}|{tape['family']}|L{tape['L']}|{tape['i']}"
   386	            if rec_key in done:
   387	                continue
   388	            t1 = time.perf_counter()
   389	            rec, arrays = H.run_policy(model, tok, pin, tape, policy, check_first=a.check_first)
   390	            np.savez_compressed(out / "runs" / f"{rec_key.replace('|', '_')}.npz", **arrays)
   391	            _append(out / "runs.jsonl", rec)
   392	            n_done += 1
   393	            log(done=rec_key, exact_state=rec["exact_state"], first=rec["first_error"],
   394	                seconds=round(time.perf_counter() - t1, 1))
   395	    bad = recount_runs(out)
   396	    atomic(out / "recount.json", {"bad": bad, "n_lines": sum(1 for ln in (out / "runs.jsonl").read_text().splitlines() if ln.strip())})
   397	    if bad:
   398	        log(recount_bad=len(bad))
   399	        raise SystemExit(4)
   400	    run = json.loads((out / "run.json").read_text())
   401	    run["status"] = "complete"
   402	    run["seconds"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)
   403	    atomic(out / "run.json", run)
   404	    log(finished=run["seconds"], wrote=n_done)
   405	
   406	
   407	def mode_e0(a):
   408	    import br_core as C
   409	    hp_heads.check()
   410	    check_adapter(a.adapter)
   411	    tok = C.tokenizer()
   412	    # a short program, only to re-check the mechanism before any E0 forward is kept
   413	    meta, _tapes = H.freeze_family(tok, "parity", lengths=(8,), n=1)
   414	    tok, model, host = _load(a.adapter)
   415	    pin = hp_attn.Pin(model)
   416	    _recheck(model, tok, pin, meta, a.out)
   417	    t0 = time.time()
   418	    rows = H.run_e0(model, tok)
   419	    path = pathlib.Path(a.out) / "e0.jsonl"
   420	    path.write_text("".join(json.dumps(r) + "\n" for r in rows))
   421	    bad = recount_e0(path)
   422	    atomic(pathlib.Path(a.out) / "e0_meta.json",
   423	           {"host": host, "seed": H.E0_SEED, "n": len(rows), "bad": bad, "adapter": a.adapter,
   424	            "seconds": round(time.time() - t0, 1)})
   425	    if bad:
   426	        raise SystemExit(4)
   427	    log(e0=len(rows), seconds=round(time.time() - t0, 1))

    43	def source_hashes(root_hp=ROOT, root_br=BR, root_pb=PB):
    44	    files = [root_hp / f for f in HP_FILES] + [root_br / f for f in BR_FILES] + sorted(root_pb.glob("pb_*.py"))
    45	    return {f.name: hashlib.sha256(f.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for f in files}

    48	def stamp():
    49	    return dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat()
    50	
    51	
    52	def rate(gpu):
    53	    cores, gib = SHAPES[gpu]
    54	    return GPU_RATE[gpu] + cores * CPU_RATE + gib * MEM_RATE

    57	@contextlib.contextmanager
    58	def ledger():
    59	    LEDGER.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    60	    lock = LEDGER.with_suffix(".lock")
    61	    with lock.open("a+") as lk:
    62	        fcntl.flock(lk, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
    63	        led = json.loads(LEDGER.read_text()) if LEDGER.exists() else {
    64	            "app": APP, "volume": VOLUME, "cap_usd": CAP_USD,
    65	            "cap_history": [{"at": stamp(), "cap_usd": CAP_USD, "why": "HEADPIN brief: cap $25 recorded before spend"}],
    66	            "cost_basis": "Modal list price upper bound: GPU + requested cores and memory per second, wall time "
    67	                          f"plus {STARTUP} s startup; not an invoice", "jobs": {}}
    68	        yield led
    69	        tmp = LEDGER.with_suffix(".tmp")
    70	        tmp.write_text(json.dumps(led, indent=1))
    71	        os.replace(tmp, LEDGER)
    72	        fcntl.flock(lk, fcntl.LOCK_UN)

    75	def used(led):
    76	    return sum(j.get("settled_usd", j["reserved_usd"]) for j in led["jobs"].values())

   101	    def _run(job):
   102	        t0 = time.monotonic()
   103	        have = source_hashes(pathlib.Path("/root/hp"), pathlib.Path("/root/br"), pathlib.Path("/root/pb"))
   104	        if have != job.get("sources"):
   105	            bad = sorted(k for k in set(have) | set(job.get("sources", {})) if have.get(k) != job["sources"].get(k))
   106	            return {"wall": round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1), "returncode": -2, "log_tail": f"source mismatch: {bad}",
   107	                    "gpu_actual": "not started", "cpu_actual": "not started"}
   108	        out_vol.reload()
   109	        for stale in pathlib.Path("/tmp").glob("*merged*"):
   110	            subprocess.run(["/bin/rm", "-rf", str(stale)])
   111	        out = pathlib.Path("/out") / job["id"]
   112	        out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   113	        cmd = [sys.executable, "-u", f"/root/hp/{job['argv'][0]}", *job["argv"][1:], "--out", str(out)]
   114	        last = time.monotonic()
   115	        with (out / "job.log").open("w") as f:
   116	            p = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdout=f, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, cwd="/root/hp",
   117	                                 env=dict(os.environ, HP_JOB_ID=job["id"]))
   118	            deadline = time.monotonic() + job["max_seconds"]
   119	            while p.poll() is None:
   120	                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
   121	                    p.kill()
   122	                    p.wait()
   123	                    break
   124	                time.sleep(10)
   125	                if time.monotonic() - last > 600:
   126	                    with contextlib.suppress(Exception):
   127	                        out_vol.commit()
   128	                    last = time.monotonic()
   129	        log = (out / "job.log").read_text()
   130	        try:
   131	            gpu = subprocess.check_output(["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=name", "--format=csv,noheader"], text=True).strip()
   132	        except Exception:
   133	            gpu = "none"
   134	        info = pathlib.Path("/proc/cpuinfo").read_text()
   135	        cpu = " | ".join([ln.split(":", 1)[1].strip() for ln in info.splitlines()
   136	                          if ln.lower().startswith(("model name", "vendor_id", "cpu family", "model\t"))][:4]) or "unknown"
   137	        flags = next((ln.split(":", 1)[1].split() for ln in info.splitlines() if ln.startswith("flags")), [])
   138	        res = {"wall": round(time.monotonic() - t0, 1), "returncode": p.returncode, "log_tail": log[-8000:],
   139	               "gpu_actual": gpu, "cpu_actual": f"{cpu} | avx512f={'avx512f' in flags}"}
   140	        (out / "_result.json").write_text(json.dumps(res))
   141	        out_vol.commit()
   142	        return res
   143	
   144	    def _kw(gpu):
   145	        cores, gib = SHAPES[gpu]
     1	# HEADPIN brief: exact execution while the model keeps its memory
     2	
     3	Neo approved this on 28 Oct 2026. The Modal cap is **$25**. Read this whole file first. Double-check everything; the
     4	rules in §5 are mandatory.
     5	
     6	## 1. The question
     7	Savant is exact today because the clock pins **every** head: the program Π stays, the history is deleted, and
     8	positions restart. That forgets the conversation. Theory round 2 proposed two ways to stay exact **while keeping the
     9	full history**:
    10	- **E1, the head-level pin.** Pin only the executor heads: the lookup committee, so that they see Π plus the current
    11	  (state, event) block. Every other head sees the full history.
    12	- **E2, bounded lag.** Keep the full history and re-insert Π every W tokens. The idea is that dilution, not memory,
    13	  breaks exactness.
    14	
    15	Plus the referee's cheapest test of the margin lemma:
    16	- **E0, delayed first use.** Teacher-force a correct trace that avoids one row r, then query r for the first time at
    17	  several delays. Compare two programs that differ only in r's destination (u vs v), and measure
    18	  S(n) = (z_u[u] − z_u[v]) − (z_v[u] − z_v[v]) and both margins. Control: move the single copy of Π near the query
    19	  while keeping total length and token counts the same.
    20	
    21	## 2. Where things are
    22	- **Worktree:** `/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/headpin`, branch `headpin` (a copy of `bridge`). Commit often. Never
    23	  push or publish.
    24	- **Code to reuse:** `research/bridge/`. BRIDGE.md, `br_core.py`, `br_free.py`, `br_tf.py` and `br_modal.py` hold
    25	  BRIDGE's plain-decoding (full history) and pinned harnesses, with events supplied, on Savant r2 and r3. Reuse them;
    26	  do not reinvent the decoding loop.
    27	- **Head lists:** the CIRCUITS report and results in `/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/anatomy-circuits/research/anatomy/`
    28	  (finder L7.H7; a read-out committee of about 30–45 heads, mostly in layers 13–29, the core in 20–27), and INSIGHTS.md
    29	  §2. Use the committee as measured there. Also run a control with the same number of random heads.
    30	- **Model:** Savant r3 (`/arvol/arith-s1r2/adapter_step500` on `polyformer-arith-results`, read-only). Merge fresh in
    31	  every job.
    32	
    33	## 3. Design (register in HEADPIN.md before spending)
    34	- **Tables and tapes:** reuse BRIDGE Part 1's tables and tapes with events supplied: 3 tables, n = 8 tapes, lengths
    35	  1,024, 4,096 and 8,192.
    36	- **Policies:**
    37	  - **pin-all:** today's clock, the reference;
    38	  - **full:** plain decoding;
    39	  - **pin-committee:** a per-head mask where committee heads see Π plus the current block, with positions handled as
    40	    in the clock, and all other heads see everything;
    41	  - **pin-random:** the same, with an equal number of random heads;
    42	  - **requote-W** for W ∈ {256, 1024}.
    43	- **Per run, record:**
    44	  - exact or not;
    45	  - the first error step and its kind (step or read);
    46	  - the minimum margin over the run.
    47	- **E0:** 4 tables × 2 destinations × delays {64, 256, 1,024, 4,096} × relocation on/off.
    48	- **Implementation note.** A per-head mask means building, at each layer, attention scores with different key sets
    49	  for different heads. The simplest correct route is a custom attention function that masks history keys for the
    50	  chosen heads. Verify on a short run that pin-committee with **all** heads chosen reproduces pin-all bitwise, and
    51	  that with **no** heads chosen it reproduces full bitwise. Both checks are mandatory before any counted run.
    52	
    53	**Suggested predictions** (adjust after the smoke run, and say why):
    54	- pin-committee is exact wherever pin-all is, up to 8,192;
    55	- pin-random is not better than full;
    56	- requote-256 beats full at 8,192;
    57	- E0's S(n) and the paired margins fall with delay, and relocation restores them.
    58	
    59	## 4. Deliverables
    60	- `research/headpin/HEADPIN.md`: a summary at the top; the predictions; a table of policies × lengths with n, seed
    61	  and host; E0's curves; "What could be wrong"; and what this means for R5, in one paragraph.
    62	- `results/` and `results/modal_ledger.json`.
    63	
    64	## 5. Rules
    65	1. Register before spend, and never edit predictions afterwards.
    66	2. Smoke first, including the two bitwise identity checks above.
    67	3. Hash the sources into the image and refuse to run on a mismatch; redeploy after every edit. Merge fresh in every
    68	   job.
    69	4. No typed numbers: every number comes from a results file via a script, and the key numbers are recounted a second
    70	   way.
    71	5. Own Modal app `polyformer-headpin`, own volume `polyformer-headpin-results`, deploy + spawn + volume. Record every
    72	   job in the ledger before spending, with GPU type and host CPU. Stop at $25.
    73	6. Keys in `~/.polyformer_keys` and the HF token: never print, log or commit them.
    74	7. Use `nice -n 19`, `/bin/rm` and `/bin/cp -f`. Never run a blocking long-lived command, and poll Modal with short
    75	   checks. Disk is tight, so do not download models locally.
    76	8. If something is ambiguous in a way that changes the science, write the question at the top of HEADPIN.md and stop.

    44	def test_e0_sequences():
    45	    states = ["a", "b", "c", "d"]
    46	    events = ["x", "y", "z"]
    47	    rows = [(s, e, states[(i + 1) % 4]) for e in events for i, s in enumerate(states)]
    48	    table = {"states": states, "events": events, "rows": rows, "shape": "perm"}
    49	    id_of = {w: i + 1 for i, w in enumerate(states + events)}
    50	    built = hp_e0.sequences(table, id_of, 99, 8, "test")
    51	    assert built["s"] == "a" and built["e"] == "z"
    52	    assert built["u"] != built["v"]
    53	    far = built["seqs"][("u", False)]["ids"]
    54	    near = built["seqs"][("u", True)]["ids"]
    55	    assert far[-2:] == [id_of["a"], id_of["z"]]
    56	    assert near[-2:] == far[-2:]
    57	    assert id_of["z"] not in far[len(built["seqs"][("u", False)]["ids"]) - 2 - 8: -2]
    58	    # the trace is the same object for both destinations: only one Π token differs
    59	    fu, fv = built["seqs"][("u", False)]["ids"], built["seqs"][("v", False)]["ids"]
    60	    assert sum(a != b for a, b in zip(fu, fv)) == 1
    61	    assert len(fu) == len(fv) == len(near)
    62	    # a second draw with the same seed is the same trace
    63	    again = hp_e0.sequences(table, id_of, 99, 8, "test")
    64	    assert again["seqs"][("u", False)]["ids"] == far
    65	    # the trace does not depend on a different destination's rng
    66	    rng = random.Random("test|8|a|z")
    67	    assert hp_e0.trace_ids(table, id_of, 8, rng) == far[len(far) - 2 - 8: -2]
    68

 ## 6. What this means for R5
 
 <!-- R5:START -->
-Pending results.
+H1 does not hold (committee state-exact wherever pin-all is: 1024: 12 of 24, 4096: 0 of 24, 8192: 0 of 24). H2 holds (pin-random state-exact counts 9, 9, 10 against full 24, 24, 22). H3 does not hold (requote-256 state-exact 0 against full 22 at 8192). H4 does not hold. For R5, neither intervention is a substitute for the clock. Pinning the destination-reading heads does not keep the tapes pin-all gets right, and a same-sized random mask is not better than leaving every head free. Putting Π back every 256 tokens does not beat the plain forward. Moving Π next to the query still raises S and both margins at delay 4096, so the table beside the query matters, but the distant medians do not fall strictly with delay on these tables.

    63	def name_pool(tok, n_train=240, n_test=120):
    64	    """Disjoint train and test names; every name stays one token beside every other name and beside a newline,
    65	    so a row's token ids are exactly (s, e, t, newline) and a block's are (s, e). Each split keeps the kinds'
    66	    proportions of the candidate list."""
    67	    nl = _ids(tok, "\n")
    68	    assert len(nl) == 1, "newline is not one token"
    69	    nl = nl[0]
    70	    cands = _candidates(tok)
    71	    cut = int(0.66 * len(cands))
    72	    id_of, pools = {}, {}
    73	    for split, src, n in (("train", cands[:cut], n_train), ("test", cands[cut:], n_test)):
    74	        chosen = []
    75	        for w, _kind in src:
    76	            if len(chosen) >= n:
    77	                break
    78	            wid = _ids(tok, " " + w)[0]
    79	            if w in id_of or wid in id_of.values():
    80	                continue
    81	            id_of[w] = wid
    82	            if _fits(tok, w, wid, [c for c in id_of if c != w], id_of, nl):
    83	                chosen.append(w)
    84	            else:
    85	                del id_of[w]
    86	        pools[split] = chosen
    87	    # Family alphabets the clock will walk by id: bare digits (Qwen splits digits, so " 1" is two tokens), single
    88	    # letters with a space, brackets. They are names by id only (no text round trip), and only in training.
    89	    for name, text in ([(d, d) for d in "0123456789"] + [(c, " " + c) for c in "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz"]
    90	                       + [(b, b) for b in "()[]"]):
    91	        ids = _ids(tok, text)
    92	        if len(ids) == 1 and ids[0] not in id_of.values() and name not in id_of:
    93	            id_of[name] = ids[0]
    94	            pools["train"].append(name)
    95	    kinds = dict(cands)
    96	    return {"train": pools["train"], "test": pools["test"], "id_of": id_of, "nl": nl,
    97	            "kinds": {s: {k: sum(kinds.get(w) == k for w in pools[s]) for k in ("lower", "cap", "code", "letter")}
    98	                      | {"extra": sum(w not in kinds for w in pools[s])} for s in ("train", "test")}}

   103	def draw_table(rng, names, n=None, a=None, shape=None):
   104	    """A total table over n states and a events (n·a ≤ 256 rows). Shapes: random destinations, a permutation per
   105	    event (swaps, moves), a bounded counter (±1 or hold), mostly self-loops (filters, flags). Rows are written
   106	    state-major (as a person writes them) or shuffled, half each."""
   107	    n = n or rng.randint(2, 32)
   108	    a = a or rng.randint(2, 8)
   109	    shape = shape or rng.choices(["random", "perm", "counter", "sticky"], weights=[0.55, 0.2, 0.1, 0.15])[0]
   110	    picked = rng.sample(list(names), n + a)
   111	    states, events = picked[:n], picked[n:]
   112	    delta = {}
   113	    for j, e in enumerate(events):
   114	        if shape == "perm":
   115	            perm = states[:]
   116	            rng.shuffle(perm)
   117	            for s, t in zip(states, perm):
   118	                delta[(s, e)] = t
   119	        elif shape == "counter":
   120	            step = rng.choice([-1, 0, 1])
   121	            for i, s in enumerate(states):
   122	                delta[(s, e)] = states[min(n - 1, max(0, i + step))]
   123	        else:
   124	            for s in states:
   125	                stay = shape == "sticky" and rng.random() < 0.8
   126	                delta[(s, e)] = s if stay else rng.choice(states)
   127	    rows = [(s, e, delta[(s, e)]) for s in states for e in events]
   128	    if rng.random() < 0.5:
   129	        rng.shuffle(rows)
   130	    return {"states": states, "events": events, "rows": rows, "shape": shape, "start": states[0]}

   133	def table_text(table):
   134	    return "".join(ROW_FMT.format(s=s, e=e, t=t) for s, e, t in table["rows"])

     1	# CIRCUITS: how Savant runs a pinned tick
     2	
     3	Branch `anatomy-circuits`, 28 Oct 2026. Uncounted exploratory study, not registered. Model: Savant r2 = Qwen/Qwen3-4B +
     4	PROMPTBOX adapter `rlg-ours/adapter_iter60`, merged in memory in every job, bf16. Earlier checkpoints come from the same
     5	PROMPTBOX volume, mounted read-only. Every GPU job ran on Modal, on an NVIDIA L4 host with CPU GenuineIntel family 6
     6	model 85 and avx512f (gVisor). Each record's `host` field carries this. Spend is in §8.
     7	
     8	The pinned tick, as PROMPTBOX §3.5 defines it:
     9	- Π is the table rows, ` s e t` plus a newline per row.
    10	- A tick is one forward over Π plus a two-token block [s][e] at positions |Π| and |Π|+1.
    11	- The next state is the argmax at the event token.
    12	
    13	Every number below gives its n and seed. Table seeds are never 101 or 777 (PROMPTBOX's validation and gate seeds).
    14	
    15	## The picture in one paragraph
    16	
    39	### 1. A weights-level certificate N\* does not exist for Savant as trained, and the reasons are measurable (Q1, Q2)
    40	
    41	**The lookup is a committee** (Fig. 1, Fig. 2; 55 held-out tables, seed 3030, 3,223 rows).
    42	- 45 of the 1,152 heads put more than 50% of the event token's attention on the matched row. This is measured on the
    43	  survey set: 76 tables, 4,356 rows, seed 2026, of which 48 are random tables and 28 are the families' literal
    44	  tables. Of the 45:
    45	  - 31 read the destination token t;
    46	  - 9 read the event token e;
    47	  - 5 read the newline.
    48	  The top heads are L24.H23 (0.97), L22.H23, L22.H22, L20.H21, L24.H22, L23.H4 and L29.H11.

   162	def spawn(job_id, gpu, max_seconds, argv, note=""):
   163	    job = {"id": job_id, "gpu": gpu, "max_seconds": max_seconds, "argv": argv, "sources": source_hashes()}
   164	    reserved = round(rate(gpu) * (max_seconds + STARTUP), 4)
   165	    commit = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=ROOT, text=True).strip()
   166	    dirty = subprocess.check_output(["git", "status", "--porcelain", "--", "."], cwd=ROOT, text=True)
   167	    dirty_src = [ln for ln in dirty.splitlines() if ln.strip().endswith(".py")]
   168	    with ledger() as led:
   169	        if job_id in led["jobs"]:
   170	            raise SystemExit(f"job {job_id} exists")
   171	        if used(led) + reserved > led["cap_usd"]:
   172	            raise SystemExit(f"cap: {used(led):.2f} used + {reserved:.2f} > {led['cap_usd']}")
   173	        led["jobs"][job_id] = {"argv": argv, "gpu": gpu, "max_seconds": max_seconds, "reserved_usd": reserved,
   174	                               "status": "reserved", "reserved_at": stamp(), "commit": commit,
   175	                               "uncommitted_sources": dirty_src, "note": note}
   176	    fn = modal.Function.from_name(APP, f"run_{gpu.lower()}")
   177	    call = fn.spawn(job)
   178	    with ledger() as led:
   179	        led["jobs"][job_id].update(call_id=call.object_id, status="running", launched_at=stamp())
   180	    print(json.dumps({"id": job_id, "call": call.object_id, "reserved_usd": reserved, "commit": commit[:10],
   181	                      "dirty": dirty_src}))

   184	def collect(only=None):
   185	    with ledger() as led:
   186	        running = {k: dict(v) for k, v in led["jobs"].items() if v["status"] == "running" and (not only or k in only)}
   187	    for jid, j in running.items():
   188	        try:
   189	            res = modal.FunctionCall.from_id(j["call_id"]).get(timeout=0)
   190	        except TimeoutError:
   191	            print(json.dumps({"id": jid, "status": "running"}))
   192	            continue
   193	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   194	            try:
   195	                vol = modal.Volume.from_name(VOLUME)
   196	                res = json.loads(b"".join(vol.read_file(f"{jid}/_result.json")))
   197	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
   198	                print(json.dumps({"id": jid, "status": "unresolved", "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {str(exc)[:200]}"}))
   199	                continue
   200	        amount = round(rate(j["gpu"]) * (min(res["wall"], j["max_seconds"] + 60) + STARTUP), 4)
   201	        status = "complete" if res["returncode"] == 0 else "error"
   202	        with ledger() as led:
   203	            led["jobs"][jid].update(status=status, settled_usd=amount, wall=res["wall"], gpu_actual=res["gpu_actual"],
   204	                                    cpu_actual=res["cpu_actual"], settled_at=stamp(), returncode=res["returncode"])
   205	        print(json.dumps({"id": jid, "status": status, "usd": amount, "gpu": res["gpu_actual"],
   206	                          "cpu": res["cpu_actual"], "returncode": res["returncode"]}))
   207	        print(res["log_tail"][-3000:])

    45	def census(tapes):
    46	    rows, over = [], []
    47	    for tape in tapes:
    48	        fit = H._fits(tape)
    49	        row = {"family": tape["family"], "L": tape["L"], "i": tape["i"], "draw": tape.get("draw", 0)}
    50	        row.update({k: v for k, v in fit.items() if k != "over"})
    51	        rows.append(row)
    52	        if fit["over"]:
    53	            over.append({"family": tape["family"], "L": tape["L"], "i": tape["i"]})
    54	    peak = 0
    55	    for row in rows:
    56	        peak = max(peak, row["full"], row["requote-256"], row["requote-1024"])
    57	    return {"cap": H.limit(), "n": len(rows), "over": over, "max_tokens": peak, "rows": rows}

codex
**The arithmetic reproduces. The main weakness is that the mandatory identity check bypasses the committee kernel. “22/24 exact” is also strictly teacher-forced state accuracy.** The report explicitly says “the true trace is teacher-forced” ([HEADPIN.md:13](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/headpin/research/headpin/HEADPIN.md:13)). No files were edited.

1. **Recomputation — OK.**

I independently read all JSONL records and decoded the corresponding NPZ arrays using Python’s standard library, without importing the reporting functions. I compared argmax IDs with target IDs, located the first incorrect token, took each run’s minimum stored margin, then aggregated counts and medians.

All **432 unique runs**, with no missing or duplicate grid entries, agree with their NPZ arrays. Every displayed result-table cell matches, including rounding. Each cell below contains values for **1,024 / 4,096 / 8,192**, with 24 runs per length.

| Policy | State-exact | Read-exact | Median minimum state margin |
|---|---|---|---|
| pin-all | 24 / 24 / 24 | — | 14.75 / 14.75 / 14.75 |
| full | 24 / 24 / 22 | 24 / 19 / 0 | 10.75 / 8 / 6.25 |
| pin-committee | 12 / 0 / 0 | 0 / 0 / 0 | 0.0625 / −4.25 / −8.625 |
| pin-random | 9 / 9 / 10 | 8 / 4 / 0 | −1.0625 / −1.375 / −1.0625 |
| requote-256 | 0 / 0 / 0 | 0 / 0 / 0 | −5.625 / −7.125 / −8.375 |
| requote-1024 | 2 / 0 / 0 | 0 / 0 / 0 | −3.5 / −7.875 / −8.0625 |

Raw policy blocks start at `results/counted/runs.jsonl:1,73,145,217,289,361`; reported rows start at `HEADPIN.md:70,73,76,79,82,85`. For example, the two full-history state failures are exactly `"n_state_wrong": 1` in compass tapes 1 and 3 ([runs.jsonl:138](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/headpin/research/headpin/results/counted/runs.jsonl:138), [runs.jsonl:140](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/headpin/research/headpin/results/counted/runs.jsonl:140)).

2. **Frozen predictions and verdicts — OK for this complete dataset; two BUGs.**

The four prediction paragraphs are unchanged from registration commit `28813e1a`. Code implements H1’s implication, H2’s `<=`, H3’s strict `>`, and H4’s conjunction of strict decline and restoration. Independent verdicts are **H1 false, H2 true, H3 false, H4 false**. Exact comparisons: `"rnd <= full"` (`hp_report.py:134`), `"r256 > full8"` (`:141`), `"seq[i] > seq[i + 1]"` (`:162`), `"falling and restored"` (`:166`); registrations at `HEADPIN.md:35,37,39,41`.

- **BUG: E0 destination differs from registration.** Report: “v is the next state in the list” (`HEADPIN.md:25`). Code: `v = next(x for x in table["states"] if x != u)` ([hp_e0.py:20](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/headpin/research/headpin/hp_e0.py:20)). This chooses the **first unequal state**. With `[a,b,c,d]` and `u=b`, it chooses `a`, not `c`. Implement the registered successor, with explicit wraparound. Existing E0 arithmetic remains correct for the executed protocol; corrected E0 values and H4 are unknown.
- **BUG: H1 does not reject missing paired runs.** It uses `both = [k for k in pin if k in com]` (`hp_report.py:123`), silently dropping missing committee results. Require the complete expected key set and uniqueness before any verdict. **Effect here: zero**, because the independently checked grid is complete.

3. **Attention intervention — implementation looks correct for counted forwards; validation BUG.**

- **Active in counted forwards: OK.** The counted path sets `pin.spec = spec` and explicitly uses `use_cache=False` ([hp_core.py:232](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/headpin/research/headpin/hp_core.py:232), `:235`). Thus the nonempty-cache bypass does not disable these measurements.
- **Qwen3 ordering/GQA/head targeting: OK by inspection.** `q_norm(q_proj(...))` and `k_norm(k_proj(...))` precede RoPE (`hp_attn.py:58,59,137,189,191`). Contiguous KV repetition at `:51`, followed by indexing at `:193,194`, implements **KV head = query head // group**. Recorded geometry is **32 query / 8 KV heads**, hence group 4 (`smoke.json:92,93`). Selection uses `module.layer_idx` (`hp_attn.py:265`), and scattering writes the selected head indices (`:201,202`).
- **SDPA versus eager: OK for the recorded setup.** The loader explicitly requests `attn_implementation="sdpa"` (`../bridge/br_core.py:164`); the custom path directly imports SDPA (`hp_attn.py:29`). This does not establish eager-backend equivalence.
- **BUG: identity validation bypasses the intervention.** For all heads, `H.forward` sets **`pin.spec = None`** and invokes a separate sliced-layer implementation ([hp_core.py:228](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/headpin/research/headpin/hp_core.py:228)). No-heads returns `orig` (`hp_attn.py:266,282`). The additional diagnostic exercises only ordinary reconstructed attention at layer 0 (`:267,268`). **No identity check exercises the subset pin kernel** at `:176` or its other-position handling at `:205`. The record confirms `"all_heads_path": "sliced_layer"` (`smoke.json:105`). This fails the brief’s requirement to validate “pin-committee with all heads chosen” (`HEADPIN_BRIEF.md:50`).
- **BUG if used with cached generation:** `if spec is None or _past_len(past_key_values) > 0: return orig(...)` ([hp_attn.py:260](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/headpin/research/headpin/hp_attn.py:260)). Cached decoding silently loses the pin. Reject unsupported cache usage or implement it correctly. **Effect on current counted numbers: zero.**

4. **Leakage and comparison fairness — OK for conditional comparisons; DESIGN FLAWS for broader claims.**

**Teacher forcing:** every full-history table uses the true trace. The shared helper constructs state labels via `run_program(...)` (`../promptbox/pb_lit.py:207`), inserts them into `run_ids` (`:247,249`), and builds `ids = prompt + prog + opener + run + closer` (`../bridge/br_core.py:108`). Scoring predicts the state after the event (`hp_core.py:342,352`). Previous correct states are therefore supplied even after hypothetical errors. The final answer field is not appended by this layout. This supports conditional next-state accuracy, **not free-running reliability**; all 24 full-history runs at 8,192 have read errors (`HEADPIN.md:75`).

**Committee selection:** independently regenerated exactly from CIRCUITS’ `mrow > 0.5` and destination-slot argmax rules (`…/anatomy/circuits/ac_mkspec.py:14,16`). No HEADPIN-outcome selection is evident. However, CIRCUITS explicitly used **Savant r2** (`…/circuits/CIRCUITS.md:3,4`), while HEADPIN loads **r3** (`HEADPIN.md:29`). Thus this tests transfer of that particular committee, not every possible untrained pin.

**Training/fairness:** all arms share one frozen, merged model: `m.eval()` and `p.requires_grad_(False)` (`../bridge/br_core.py:168,170`), then iterate the same tapes (`hp_run.py:383,384`). There is no arm-specific rank, learning rate, training-step count, loss schedule, or checkpoint selection. Upstream checkpoint-training contamination is **not established absent** by these evaluation records. Programs explicitly use `split="train"` (`hp_core.py:174`); E0 uses `pool["train"]` (`:416`). These are not unseen-program/name generalization tests.

**Requote:** inserts only row tokens `[s,e,t,newline]` (`hp_core.py:80,139`) every W **original tokens after Π**, including intervening program text (`hp_seq.py:23,32,33`). Scoring correctly uses the position immediately before each original target (`hp_core.py:376,377`). It changes sequence length and format, so it does not isolate lag at equal token/compute budget. Likewise, random control matches head count, not layer distribution (`hp_heads.py:19,29`). These are limits on mechanism claims, not recount errors.

5. **Figures/captions — OK for supplied tables; curves missing.**

There are no figures in the report to validate. Table aggregation matches the captions: “median across tapes of each tape’s minimum state-token margin” (`HEADPIN.md:66`; implemented at `hp_report.py:186`). E0 medians also match. The brief requested “E0’s curves” (`HEADPIN_BRIEF.md:61`), but the delivered report contains a numerical table (`HEADPIN.md:91`).

**BUGs by severity:** high—identity gate bypasses the committee kernel; medium—E0 destination protocol mismatch; medium but dormant—cached decoding bypass; low and dormant—incomplete-grid H1 acceptance. Only the E0 correction necessarily requires new numerical measurements; the committee correction’s numerical effect is unknown until its actual kernel is validated.

**Headline claims that survive:**

- **Full history 22/24 at 8,192:** survives as teacher-forced state-exactness only.
- **Requote-256 does not beat full:** survives for this exact insertion protocol: 0/24 versus 22/24.
- **Committee pin does not beat full:** recorded counts survive; attribution to a correctly validated intervention remains provisional.
- **No untrained head pin can help:** unsupported; only one transferred committee and one random set were tested.
- **H4 fails:** survives for the executed E0 destination rule, pending correction to the registered rule.

All **432 unique runs**, with no missing or duplicate grid entries, agree with their NPZ arrays. Every displayed result-table cell matches, including rounding. Each cell below contains values for **1,024 / 4,096 / 8,192**, with 24 runs per length.

2. **Frozen predictions and verdicts — OK for this complete dataset; two BUGs.**

The four prediction paragraphs are unchanged from registration commit `28813e1a`. Code implements H1’s implication, H2’s `<=`, H3’s strict `>`, and H4’s conjunction of strict decline and restoration. Independent verdicts are **H1 false, H2 true, H3 false, H4 false**. Exact comparisons: `"rnd <= full"` (`hp_report.py:134`), `"r256 > full8"` (`:141`), `"seq[i] > seq[i + 1]"` (`:162`), `"falling and restored"` (`:166`); registrations at `HEADPIN.md:35,37,39,41`.

- **BUG: E0 destination differs from registration.** Report: “v is the next state in the list” (`HEADPIN.md:25`). Code: `v = next(x for x in table["states"] if x != u)` ([hp_e0.py:20](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/headpin/research/headpin/hp_e0.py:20)). This chooses the **first unequal state**. With `[a,b,c,d]` and `u=b`, it chooses `a`, not `c`. Implement the registered successor, with explicit wraparound. Existing E0 arithmetic remains correct for the executed protocol; corrected E0 values and H4 are unknown.
- **BUG: H1 does not reject missing paired runs.** It uses `both = [k for k in pin if k in com]` (`hp_report.py:123`), silently dropping missing committee results. Require the complete expected key set and uniqueness before any verdict. **Effect here: zero**, because the independently checked grid is complete.

3. **Attention intervention — implementation looks correct for counted forwards; validation BUG.**

- **Active in counted forwards: OK.** The counted path sets `pin.spec = spec` and explicitly uses `use_cache=False` ([hp_core.py:232](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/headpin/research/headpin/hp_core.py:232), `:235`). Thus the nonempty-cache bypass does not disable these measurements.
- **Qwen3 ordering/GQA/head targeting: OK by inspection.** `q_norm(q_proj(...))` and `k_norm(k_proj(...))` precede RoPE (`hp_attn.py:58,59,137,189,191`). Contiguous KV repetition at `:51`, followed by indexing at `:193,194`, implements **KV head = query head // group**. Recorded geometry is **32 query / 8 KV heads**, hence group 4 (`smoke.json:92,93`). Selection uses `module.layer_idx` (`hp_attn.py:265`), and scattering writes the selected head indices (`:201,202`).
- **SDPA versus eager: OK for the recorded setup.** The loader explicitly requests `attn_implementation="sdpa"` (`../bridge/br_core.py:164`); the custom path directly imports SDPA (`hp_attn.py:29`). This does not establish eager-backend equivalence.
- **BUG: identity validation bypasses the intervention.** For all heads, `H.forward` sets **`pin.spec = None`** and invokes a separate sliced-layer implementation ([hp_core.py:228](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/headpin/research/headpin/hp_core.py:228)). No-heads returns `orig` (`hp_attn.py:266,282`). The additional diagnostic exercises only ordinary reconstructed attention at layer 0 (`:267,268`). **No identity check exercises the subset pin kernel** at `:176` or its other-position handling at `:205`. The record confirms `"all_heads_path": "sliced_layer"` (`smoke.json:105`). This fails the brief’s requirement to validate “pin-committee with all heads chosen” (`HEADPIN_BRIEF.md:50`).
- **BUG if used with cached generation:** `if spec is None or _past_len(past_key_values) > 0: return orig(...)` ([hp_attn.py:260](/Users/neo/repos/polyformer-wt/headpin/research/headpin/hp_attn.py:260)). Cached decoding silently loses the pin. Reject unsupported cache usage or implement it correctly. **Effect on current counted numbers: zero.**

4. **Leakage and comparison fairness — OK for conditional comparisons; DESIGN FLAWS for broader claims.**

**Teacher forcing:** every full-history table uses the true trace. The shared helper constructs state labels via `run_program(...)` (`../promptbox/pb_lit.py:207`), inserts them into `run_ids` (`:247,249`), and builds `ids = prompt + prog + opener + run + closer` (`../bridge/br_core.py:108`). Scoring predicts the state after the event (`hp_core.py:342,352`). Previous correct states are therefore supplied even after hypothetical errors. The final answer field is not appended by this layout. This supports conditional next-state accuracy, **not free-running reliability**; all 24 full-history runs at 8,192 have read errors (`HEADPIN.md:75`).

**Committee selection:** independently regenerated exactly from CIRCUITS’ `mrow > 0.5` and destination-slot argmax rules (`…/anatomy/circuits/ac_mkspec.py:14,16`). No HEADPIN-outcome selection is evident. However, CIRCUITS explicitly used **Savant r2** (`…/circuits/CIRCUITS.md:3,4`), while HEADPIN loads **r3** (`HEADPIN.md:29`). Thus this tests transfer of that particular committee, not every possible untrained pin.

**Training/fairness:** all arms share one frozen, merged model: `m.eval()` and `p.requires_grad_(False)` (`../bridge/br_core.py:168,170`), then iterate the same tapes (`hp_run.py:383,384`). There is no arm-specific rank, learning rate, training-step count, loss schedule, or checkpoint selection. Upstream checkpoint-training contamination is **not established absent** by these evaluation records. Programs explicitly use `split="train"` (`hp_core.py:174`); E0 uses `pool["train"]` (`:416`). These are not unseen-program/name generalization tests.

**Requote:** inserts only row tokens `[s,e,t,newline]` (`hp_core.py:80,139`) every W **original tokens after Π**, including intervening program text (`hp_seq.py:23,32,33`). Scoring correctly uses the position immediately before each original target (`hp_core.py:376,377`). It changes sequence length and format, so it does not isolate lag at equal token/compute budget. Likewise, random control matches head count, not layer distribution (`hp_heads.py:19,29`). These are limits on mechanism claims, not recount errors.

5. **Figures/captions — OK for supplied tables; curves missing.**

There are no figures in the report to validate. Table aggregation matches the captions: “median across tapes of each tape’s minimum state-token margin” (`HEADPIN.md:66`; implemented at `hp_report.py:186`). E0 medians also match. The brief requested “E0’s curves” (`HEADPIN_BRIEF.md:61`), but the delivered report contains a numerical table (`HEADPIN.md:91`).
